## AskUserQuestion Format

### Tool resolution (read first)

"AskUserQuestion" can resolve to two tools at runtime: the **host MCP variant** (e.g. `mcp__conductor__AskUserQuestion` — appears in your tool list when the host registers it) or the **native** Claude Code tool.

**Rule:** if any `mcp__*__AskUserQuestion` variant is in your tool list, prefer it. Hosts may disable native AUQ via `--disallowedTools AskUserQuestion` (Conductor does, by default) and route through their MCP variant; calling native there silently fails. Same questions/options shape; same decision-brief format applies.

**Fallback when neither variant is callable:** in plan mode, write the decision brief into the plan file as a `## Decisions to confirm` section + ExitPlanMode (the native "Ready to execute?" surfaces it). Outside plan mode, output the brief as prose and stop. **Never silently auto-decide** — only `/plan-tune` AUTO_DECIDE opt-ins authorize auto-picking.

### Format

Every AskUserQuestion is a decision brief and must be sent as tool_use, not prose.

```
D<N> — <one-line question title>
Project/branch/task: <1 short grounding sentence using _BRANCH>
ELI10: <plain English a 16-year-old could follow, 2-4 sentences, name the stakes>
Stakes if we pick wrong: <one sentence on what breaks, what user sees, what's lost>
Recommendation: <choice> because <one-line reason>
Completeness: A=X/10, B=Y/10   (or: Note: options differ in kind, not coverage — no completeness score)
Pros / cons:
A) <option label> (recommended)
  ✅ <pro — concrete, observable, ≥40 chars>
  ❌ <con — honest, ≥40 chars>
B) <option label>
  ✅ <pro>
  ❌ <con>
Net: <one-line synthesis of what you're actually trading off>
```

D-numbering: first question in a skill invocation is `D1`; increment yourself. This is a model-level instruction, not a runtime counter.

ELI10 is always present, in plain English, not function names. Recommendation is ALWAYS present. Keep the `(recommended)` label; AUTO_DECIDE depends on it.

Completeness: use `Completeness: N/10` only when options differ in coverage. 10 = complete, 7 = happy path, 3 = shortcut. If options differ in kind, write: `Note: options differ in kind, not coverage — no completeness score.`

Pros / cons: use ✅ and ❌. Minimum 2 pros and 1 con per option when the choice is real; Minimum 40 characters per bullet. Hard-stop escape for one-way/destructive confirmations: `✅ No cons — this is a hard-stop choice`.

Neutral posture: `Recommendation: <default> — this is a taste call, no strong preference either way`; `(recommended)` STAYS on the default option for AUTO_DECIDE.

Effort both-scales: when an option involves effort, label both human-team and CC+gstack time, e.g. `(human: ~2 days / CC: ~15 min)`. Makes AI compression visible at decision time.

Net line closes the tradeoff. Per-skill instructions may add stricter rules.

### Self-check before emitting

Before calling AskUserQuestion, verify:
- [ ] D<N> header present
- [ ] ELI10 paragraph present (stakes line too)
- [ ] Recommendation line present with concrete reason
- [ ] Completeness scored (coverage) OR kind-note present (kind)
- [ ] Every option has ≥2 ✅ and ≥1 ❌, each ≥40 chars (or hard-stop escape)
- [ ] (recommended) label on one option (even for neutral-posture)
- [ ] Dual-scale effort labels on effort-bearing options (human / CC)
- [ ] Net line closes the decision
- [ ] You are calling the tool, not writing prose


## Artifacts Sync (skill start)

```bash
_GSTACK_HOME="${GSTACK_HOME:-$HOME/.gstack}"
# Prefer the v1.27.0.0 artifacts file; fall back to brain file for users
# upgrading mid-stream before the migration script runs.
if [ -f "$HOME/.gstack-artifacts-remote.txt" ]; then
  _BRAIN_REMOTE_FILE="$HOME/.gstack-artifacts-remote.txt"
else
  _BRAIN_REMOTE_FILE="$HOME/.gstack-brain-remote.txt"
fi
_BRAIN_SYNC_BIN="$GSTACK_BIN/gstack-brain-sync"
_BRAIN_CONFIG_BIN="$GSTACK_BIN/gstack-config"

# /sync-gbrain context-load: teach the agent to use gbrain when it's available.
# Mutually exclusive variants per /plan-eng-review §4. Empty string when gbrain
# is not configured (zero context cost for non-gbrain users).
_GBRAIN_CONFIG="$HOME/.gbrain/config.json"
if [ -f "$_GBRAIN_CONFIG" ] && command -v gbrain >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  _GBRAIN_VERSION_OK=$(gbrain --version 2>/dev/null | grep -c '^gbrain ' || echo 0)
  if [ "$_GBRAIN_VERSION_OK" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
    _SYNC_STATE="$_GSTACK_HOME/.gbrain-sync-state.json"
    _CWD_PAGES=0
    if [ -f "$_SYNC_STATE" ]; then
      # Flatten newlines so the regex works against pretty-printed JSON too.
      _CWD_PAGES=$(tr -d '\n' < "$_SYNC_STATE" 2>/dev/null \
        | grep -o '"name": *"code"[^}]*"detail": *{[^}]*"page_count": *[0-9]*' \
        | grep -o '"page_count": *[0-9]*' | grep -o '[0-9]\+' | head -1)
      _CWD_PAGES=${_CWD_PAGES:-0}
    fi
    if [ "$_CWD_PAGES" -gt 0 ] 2>/dev/null; then
      echo "GBrain configured. Prefer \`gbrain search\`/\`gbrain query\` over Grep for"
      echo "semantic questions; use \`gbrain code-def\`/\`code-refs\`/\`code-callers\` for"
      echo "symbol-aware code lookup. See \"## GBrain Search Guidance\" in CLAUDE.md."
      echo "Run /sync-gbrain to refresh."
    else
      echo "GBrain configured but this repo isn't indexed yet. Run \`/sync-gbrain --full\`"
      echo "before relying on \`gbrain search\` for code questions in this repo."
      echo "Falls back to Grep until indexed."
    fi
  fi
fi

_BRAIN_SYNC_MODE=$("$_BRAIN_CONFIG_BIN" get artifacts_sync_mode 2>/dev/null || echo off)

# Detect remote-MCP mode (Path 4 of /setup-gbrain). Local artifacts sync is
# a no-op in remote mode; the brain server pulls from GitHub/GitLab on its
# own cadence. Read claude.json directly to keep this preamble fast (no
# subprocess to claude CLI on every skill start).
_GBRAIN_MCP_MODE="none"
if command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$HOME/.claude.json" ]; then
  _GBRAIN_MCP_TYPE=$(jq -r '.mcpServers.gbrain.type // .mcpServers.gbrain.transport // empty' "$HOME/.claude.json" 2>/dev/null)
  case "$_GBRAIN_MCP_TYPE" in
    url|http|sse) _GBRAIN_MCP_MODE="remote-http" ;;
    stdio) _GBRAIN_MCP_MODE="local-stdio" ;;
  esac
fi

if [ -f "$_BRAIN_REMOTE_FILE" ] && [ ! -d "$_GSTACK_HOME/.git" ] && [ "$_BRAIN_SYNC_MODE" = "off" ]; then
  _BRAIN_NEW_URL=$(head -1 "$_BRAIN_REMOTE_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[:space:]')
  if [ -n "$_BRAIN_NEW_URL" ]; then
    echo "ARTIFACTS_SYNC: artifacts repo detected: $_BRAIN_NEW_URL"
    echo "ARTIFACTS_SYNC: run 'gstack-brain-restore' to pull your cross-machine artifacts (or 'gstack-config set artifacts_sync_mode off' to dismiss forever)"
  fi
fi

if [ -d "$_GSTACK_HOME/.git" ] && [ "$_BRAIN_SYNC_MODE" != "off" ]; then
  _BRAIN_LAST_PULL_FILE="$_GSTACK_HOME/.brain-last-pull"
  _BRAIN_NOW=$(date +%s)
  _BRAIN_DO_PULL=1
  if [ -f "$_BRAIN_LAST_PULL_FILE" ]; then
    _BRAIN_LAST=$(cat "$_BRAIN_LAST_PULL_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    _BRAIN_AGE=$(( _BRAIN_NOW - _BRAIN_LAST ))
    [ "$_BRAIN_AGE" -lt 86400 ] && _BRAIN_DO_PULL=0
  fi
  if [ "$_BRAIN_DO_PULL" = "1" ]; then
    ( cd "$_GSTACK_HOME" && git fetch origin >/dev/null 2>&1 && git merge --ff-only "origin/$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)" >/dev/null 2>&1 ) || true
    echo "$_BRAIN_NOW" > "$_BRAIN_LAST_PULL_FILE"
  fi
  "$_BRAIN_SYNC_BIN" --once 2>/dev/null || true
fi

if [ "$_GBRAIN_MCP_MODE" = "remote-http" ]; then
  # Remote-MCP mode: local artifacts sync is a no-op (brain admin's server
  # pulls from GitHub/GitLab). Show the user this is by design, not broken.
  _GBRAIN_HOST=$(jq -r '.mcpServers.gbrain.url // empty' "$HOME/.claude.json" 2>/dev/null | sed -E 's|^https?://([^/:]+).*|\1|')
  echo "ARTIFACTS_SYNC: remote-mode (managed by brain server ${_GBRAIN_HOST:-remote})"
elif [ -d "$_GSTACK_HOME/.git" ] && [ "$_BRAIN_SYNC_MODE" != "off" ]; then
  _BRAIN_QUEUE_DEPTH=0
  [ -f "$_GSTACK_HOME/.brain-queue.jsonl" ] && _BRAIN_QUEUE_DEPTH=$(wc -l < "$_GSTACK_HOME/.brain-queue.jsonl" | tr -d ' ')
  _BRAIN_LAST_PUSH="never"
  [ -f "$_GSTACK_HOME/.brain-last-push" ] && _BRAIN_LAST_PUSH=$(cat "$_GSTACK_HOME/.brain-last-push" 2>/dev/null || echo never)
  echo "ARTIFACTS_SYNC: mode=$_BRAIN_SYNC_MODE | last_push=$_BRAIN_LAST_PUSH | queue=$_BRAIN_QUEUE_DEPTH"
else
  echo "ARTIFACTS_SYNC: off"
fi
```



Privacy stop-gate: if output shows `ARTIFACTS_SYNC: off`, `artifacts_sync_mode_prompted` is `false`, and gbrain is on PATH or `gbrain doctor --fast --json` works, ask once:

> gstack can publish your artifacts (CEO plans, designs, reports) to a private GitHub repo that GBrain indexes across machines. How much should sync?

Options:
- A) Everything allowlisted (recommended)
- B) Only artifacts
- C) Decline, keep everything local

After answer:

```bash
# Chosen mode: full | artifacts-only | off
"$_BRAIN_CONFIG_BIN" set artifacts_sync_mode <choice>
"$_BRAIN_CONFIG_BIN" set artifacts_sync_mode_prompted true
```

If A/B and `~/.gstack/.git` is missing, ask whether to run `gstack-artifacts-init`. Do not block the skill.

At skill END before telemetry:

```bash
"$GSTACK_BIN/gstack-brain-sync" --discover-new 2>/dev/null || true
"$GSTACK_BIN/gstack-brain-sync" --once 2>/dev/null || true
```


## Model-Specific Behavioral Patch (claude)

The following nudges are tuned for the claude model family. They are
**subordinate** to skill workflow, STOP points, AskUserQuestion gates, plan-mode
safety, and /ship review gates. If a nudge below conflicts with skill instructions,
the skill wins. Treat these as preferences, not rules.

**Todo-list discipline.** When working through a multi-step plan, mark each task
complete individually as you finish it. Do not batch-complete at the end. If a task
turns out to be unnecessary, mark it skipped with a one-line reason.

**Think before heavy actions.** For complex operations (refactors, migrations,
non-trivial new features), briefly state your approach before executing. This lets
the user course-correct cheaply instead of mid-flight.

**Dedicated tools over Bash.** Prefer Read, Edit, Write, Glob, Grep over shell
equivalents (cat, sed, find, grep). The dedicated tools are cheaper and clearer.

## Voice

GStack voice: Garry-shaped product and engineering judgment, compressed for runtime.

- Lead with the point. Say what it does, why it matters, and what changes for the builder.
- Be concrete. Name files, functions, line numbers, commands, outputs, evals, and real numbers.
- Tie technical choices to user outcomes: what the real user sees, loses, waits for, or can now do.
- Be direct about quality. Bugs matter. Edge cases matter. Fix the whole thing, not the demo path.
- Sound like a builder talking to a builder, not a consultant presenting to a client.
- Never corporate, academic, PR, or hype. Avoid filler, throat-clearing, generic optimism, and founder cosplay.
- No em dashes. No AI vocabulary: delve, crucial, robust, comprehensive, nuanced, multifaceted, furthermore, moreover, additionally, pivotal, landscape, tapestry, underscore, foster, showcase, intricate, vibrant, fundamental, significant.
- The user has context you do not: domain knowledge, timing, relationships, taste. Cross-model agreement is a recommendation, not a decision. The user decides.

Good: "auth.ts:47 returns undefined when the session cookie expires. Users hit a white screen. Fix: add a null check and redirect to /login. Two lines."
Bad: "I've identified a potential issue in the authentication flow that may cause problems under certain conditions."

## Context Recovery

At session start or after compaction, recover recent project context.

```bash
eval "$($GSTACK_BIN/gstack-slug 2>/dev/null)"
_PROJ="${GSTACK_HOME:-$HOME/.gstack}/projects/${SLUG:-unknown}"
if [ -d "$_PROJ" ]; then
  echo "--- RECENT ARTIFACTS ---"
  find "$_PROJ/ceo-plans" "$_PROJ/checkpoints" -type f -name "*.md" 2>/dev/null | xargs ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -3
  [ -f "$_PROJ/${_BRANCH}-reviews.jsonl" ] && echo "REVIEWS: $(wc -l < "$_PROJ/${_BRANCH}-reviews.jsonl" | tr -d ' ') entries"
  [ -f "$_PROJ/timeline.jsonl" ] && tail -5 "$_PROJ/timeline.jsonl"
  if [ -f "$_PROJ/timeline.jsonl" ]; then
    _LAST=$(grep "\"branch\":\"${_BRANCH}\"" "$_PROJ/timeline.jsonl" 2>/dev/null | grep '"event":"completed"' | tail -1)
    [ -n "$_LAST" ] && echo "LAST_SESSION: $_LAST"
    _RECENT_SKILLS=$(grep "\"branch\":\"${_BRANCH}\"" "$_PROJ/timeline.jsonl" 2>/dev/null | grep '"event":"completed"' | tail -3 | grep -o '"skill":"[^"]*"' | sed 's/"skill":"//;s/"//' | tr '\n' ',')
    [ -n "$_RECENT_SKILLS" ] && echo "RECENT_PATTERN: $_RECENT_SKILLS"
  fi
  _LATEST_CP=$(find "$_PROJ/checkpoints" -name "*.md" -type f 2>/dev/null | xargs ls -t 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  [ -n "$_LATEST_CP" ] && echo "LATEST_CHECKPOINT: $_LATEST_CP"
  echo "--- END ARTIFACTS ---"
fi
```

If artifacts are listed, read the newest useful one. If `LAST_SESSION` or `LATEST_CHECKPOINT` appears, give a 2-sentence welcome back summary. If `RECENT_PATTERN` clearly implies a next skill, suggest it once.

## Writing Style (skip entirely if `EXPLAIN_LEVEL: terse` appears in the preamble echo OR the user's current message explicitly requests terse / no-explanations output)

Applies to AskUserQuestion, user replies, and findings. AskUserQuestion Format is structure; this is prose quality.

- Gloss curated jargon on first use per skill invocation, even if the user pasted the term.
- Frame questions in outcome terms: what pain is avoided, what capability unlocks, what user experience changes.
- Use short sentences, concrete nouns, active voice.
- Close decisions with user impact: what the user sees, waits for, loses, or gains.

    20	must decide:**
    21	
    22	1. **Item 32 (Sprint) is blocked by a missing `<script>` tag.** The app shell
    23	   `_SHELL` in `src/planner/core/server.py` (lines 57–66) loads `screens-day.js`,
    24	   `screens-review.js`, `screens-board.js`, `screens-ticket.js` — but **not**
    25	   `screens-sprint.js` (which exists and is functional in `assets/`). Navigating to
    26	   `#/sprint` therefore renders app.js's placeholder `"not built yet"`
    27	   (`assets/app.js:28-32`), so the ready selector `[data-status-group="active"]`
    28	   never appears and item 32 hard-fails at `open_page`.
    29	   **Required one-line edit (server.py, after line 65):**
    30	   ```
    31	   <script src="/assets/screens-ticket.js"></script>
    32	   <script src="/assets/screens-sprint.js"></script>   <!-- ADD THIS LINE -->
    33	   ```
    34	   Write the item-32 test as specified below; it passes once, and only once, this
    35	   line is added. (Item 33, a different ticket, will additionally need
    36	   `screens-backlog.js`; out of scope here.)
    37	
    38	2. **Item 30's "propose result *with the claim env*" cannot be honoured in-fence, and
    39	   `plan run close` is impossible.** The dispatcher mints the claim token
    40	   (`new_claim()`, random, `dispatch/data.py:53`) into `tickets.claim_lock`, but **no
    41	   HTTP/CLI/test surface ever returns it**: `ticket_json` deliberately omits
    42	   `claim_lock` (`tickets/views.py:39-40`); the tick-dispatcher report's `spawned`
    43	   entries are `{ticket_id, run_id, pid}` only (`dispatch/runtime.py:197-199`); run
    44	   JSON and events carry no token. So a test can obtain `PLAN_RUN_ID` (from the report)
    45	   but never `PLAN_CLAIM`. Consequently `plan run close` — whose route calls
    46	   `require_claim` unconditionally (`dispatch/api.py:49`) — is unreachable, and a
    47	   *claimed* `propose result` is unreachable.
    48	   **Resolution (SPEC-sanctioned, in-fence):** the dispatched-worker's `propose result`
    49	   is issued **claimless** — the CLI sends only `X-Plan-Actor: agent`
    50	   (`cli/http.py:35`), which `authctx` classifies as a *non-dispatched agent context*
    51	   (`is_claimed_agent=False`), so `propose_field` skips `require_claim`
    52	   (`tickets/api.py:296-297`) and files a normal agent proposal. This is exactly the
    53	   §7.6 exception ("proposal/recap writes from non-dispatched agent contexts … are
    54	   permitted"). It reaches every **scored** assertion of SPEC item 30 (line 309):
    55	   dispatcher claims it (run visible on Ticket), result parks in `needs_review`,
    56	   appears in the approval queue, approve → `done`. The `plan run close` sub-step
    57	   named only in the *ticket* (not the SPEC item) is **omitted**; note this in
    58	   `decisions.md`. If literal claim-env fidelity is wanted later, it requires a new
    59	   test-mode seam (e.g. add `claim` to the report's `spawned` entries, or a
    60	   `GET /api/test/claim/{ticket_id}`) — a separate src ticket.

    64	## 1. Harness recap (reuse, do not reinvent)
    65	
    66	From `tests/e2e/conftest.py`:
    67	- `server` — one fresh `plan serve` subprocess + fresh temp SQLite DB per test,
    68	  `PLAN_TEST_MODE=1`, `PLAN_FAKE_NOW=2026-07-04T12:00:00`, `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS=50`,
    69	  `PLAN_UI_DEBOUNCE_MS=50`, echo gateway, **fake** spawn/boundary adapters (auto→fake
    70	  under test mode, `registry.py:35-58`). **`server` alone suffices for all five tests**
    71	  — fresh DB, no cross-test state; `server_factory` is not needed.
    72	- `open_page(ctx, server, route, ready_selector, settled)` — goes to
    73	  `server.base + "/" + route`, waits `ready_selector`, waits `wsOpens>=1`; if
    74	  `settled=True` also waits `flushes>=1` then re-anchors `ready_selector`. **Use
    75	  `settled=True` whenever the DB state was mutated before the page opened** (all five
    76	  tests open their pages after setup).
    77	- `cli(server, *args, ticket_id=?, actor=?, run_id=?, claim=?, stdin=?)` — runs
    78	  `plan … --json`, asserts rc==0, returns parsed JSON. Sets `PLAN_TICKET_ID`/
    79	  `PLAN_ACTOR`/`PLAN_RUN_ID`/`PLAN_CLAIM` env only when the kwarg is given.
    80	- `api.get(server, path)` → GET JSON (asserts <300). `api.human_post(server, path,
    81	  json_body)` → POST JSON with **no** `X-Plan-*` headers (classified as the human;
    82	  also the correct caller for the `/api/test/*` endpoints, which do no auth).
    83	
    84	Module top matches flows_a: `from __future__ import annotations`, `from
    85	playwright.sync_api import Page`, `WAIT_MS = 10_000`, and a module docstring (see §3).
    86	
    87	**Fence rules (SPEC §18.2 / ticket):** exactly ONE test per anchor
    88	`test_e28_`…`test_e32_` across the whole suite; every shared helper has a non-`test_`
    89	name; no `pytest.skip`/`xfail`/`.only`/commented-out/empty bodies; no `parametrize` on
    90	anchored tests. Assert SPEC-exact literals, never weakened.

   145	## 3. Module docstring (mirror flows_a's discipline)
   146	
   147	```
   148	"""E2E flows B — SPEC §18.3 items 28-32 (Playwright, chromium; two contexts in e32).
   149	
   150	One test per acceptance item, its name carrying the ``test_eNN_`` anchor the verify
   151	scorer matches: exactly one anchored match per item across the whole e2e suite, so no
   152	parametrize and every shared helper below has a non-``test_`` name. Boundary/dispatch
   153	ticks are driven synchronously through /api/test/*; time through /api/test/set-now.
   154	Assertions use the SPEC's exact values (planning dates, fake-adapter strings, node
   155	statuses, ticket states); every wait carries an explicit timeout and precedes its
   156	assert — no sleeps."""
   157	```
   158

   160	
   161	## 4. Shared non-`test_` helpers
   162	
   163	```python
   164	def _set_now(api, server, iso):
   165	    r = api.human_post(server, "/api/test/set-now", {"now": iso})
   166	    return r  # {"now":..., "planning_date":...}
   167	
   168	def _tick_boundary(api, server):
   169	    return api.human_post(server, "/api/test/tick-boundary", {})   # pinned 4-key report
   170	
   171	def _tick_dispatcher(api, server):
   172	    return api.human_post(server, "/api/test/tick-dispatcher", {})  # pinned 5-key report
   173	
   174	def _wait_node_note(page, node, text):
   175	    page.wait_for_function(
   176	        "a => { const n = document.querySelector('[data-node=\"'+a.node+'\"] .plan-node-note');"
   177	        " return !!n && n.textContent === a.text; }",
   178	        arg={"node": str(node), "text": text}, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   179	
   180	def _grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, tid, ceiling):
   181	    # Human grant (header-less → human; grant_ticket rejects agents, tickets/api.py:359).
   182	    g = api.human_post(server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}/grant",
   183	                       {"ceiling": ceiling, "at_cap": "propose"})
   184	    assert g["ceiling"] == ceiling and g["at_cap"] == "propose", g
   185	    # Claimless CLI proposals auto-accept up the chain to in_progress (like flows_a e27).
   186	    for field, body in (("success", ...), ("approach", ...), ("plan", ...)):
   187	        cli(server, "propose", field, "--body-file", "-", ticket_id=tid, stdin=body)
   188	    d = api.get(server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}")
   189	    assert d["state"] == "in_progress", d
   190	    return d
   191	
   192	def _reload_settle(page, ready_selector):
   193	    # open_page opens FRESH pages; after page.reload() re-apply its wait discipline.
   194	    page.reload()
   195	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   196	    page.wait_for_function(
   197	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1", timeout=WAIT_MS)
   198	```
   199	(`_grant_and_advance` bodies come from the caller's E30_*/E31_* constants; inline the
   200	three-item loop with the right constants per test, or pass a mapping — implementer's
   201	choice, but keep it one helper, non-`test_`.)
   202

   204	
   205	## 5. Per-test blueprints
   206	
   207	Screen/route facts (from `assets/`): Day route `#/day`, screen root
   208	`section[data-screen="day"]` set by `screens-day.js:23`; Board `#/board`,
   209	`section[data-screen="board"]`; Ticket `#/ticket/<id>`,
   210	`section[data-screen="ticket"][data-ticket-id][data-state]` (`screens-ticket.js:427-430`);
   211	Review `#/review`; Sprint `#/sprint`, root `[data-screen="sprint"]` (a `div`, set
   212	`screens-sprint.js:313`). The `open_page` `route` arg includes the leading `#`
   213	(e.g. `"#/day"`), exactly as flows_a passes it.
   214	
   215	### test_e28_day_boundary_accept_all(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   216	
   217	Satisfies SPEC line 307: "driving `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` at fake-now 05:01
   218	produces the brief and a proposed tree; Accept-all adds the child tickets to the day
   219	list." Guarantees: boundary job §6.2 (`days/boundary.py:34-90`), carryover
   220	(`carryover.py:20-36`), fake judgment children-from-carryover (`fakes.py:50-62`),
   221	tree render (`components.js:558-617`), accept-all + AddTicketToDay (§6.3 line 137,
   222	`tree.py:101-114`, `days/data.py:185-207`).
   223	
   224	1. `tid = cli(server, "ticket", "create", "--title", E28_TITLE)["id"]` — created
   225	   `needs_success`, non-`done` → a carryover candidate.
   226	2. Put it on the **previous** day (planning date is 2026-07-04 at baseline; use the
   227	   explicit date to be deterministic):
   228	   `cli(server, "day", "add-ticket", tid, DAY_PREV)` → `POST /api/day/2026-07-04/tickets`
   229	   (`cli/main.py:514-520`; route materializes the day, `days/api.py:129-137`). This is
   230	   yesterday's day-ticket, NOT a plan of 2026-07-05, so the §6.2 human-planned skip
   231	   (`boundary.py:170-182`) is not triggered.
   232	3. `_set_now(api, server, NOW_0501)` → assert returned `planning_date == DAY_CUR`.
   233	4. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`. **Assert the pinned report**
   234	   (`scheduler.py:268-273`): `rep == {"planning_date": DAY_CUR, "ran": True,
   235	   "judgment": "ok", "replan": None}`. (`ran` True: first tick for 2026-07-05;
   236	   `judgment` "ok": success path recorded `boundary.py:90`.)
   237	5. `page = open_page(context_factory(), server, "#/day",
   238	   'section[data-screen="day"]', settled=True)`.
   239	6. **Brief** (§6.2 stored brief → `markdownBlock`): assert
   240	   `page.inner_text('.day-main .markdown-block h1') == FAKE_BRIEF_H1`.
   241	   (`# Brief for 2026-07-05` → `<h1>`, `markdown.js:124-129`; day-main's only
   242	   markdown-block, `screens-day.js:113`.)
   243	7. **Proposed tree** (`planTree`): wait `page.wait_for_selector('.plan-tree [data-node="0"]',
   244	   timeout=WAIT_MS)`, then assert:
   245	   - `page.inner_text('.plan-tree [data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus') == FAKE_FOCUS`
   246	   - `page.get_attribute('.plan-tree [data-node="root"]', "data-status") == "proposed"`
   247	   - `page.inner_text('.plan-tree [data-node="0"] .plan-node-note') == E28_TITLE`
   248	     (child note = carryover title, `fakes.py:53`)
   249	   - `page.get_attribute('.plan-tree [data-node="0"]', "data-status") == "proposed"`

   263	### test_e29_invalidation_root_and_child(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   264	
   265	Satisfies SPEC line 308: root invalidate → replacement tree; child invalidate →
   266	only that child replaced, others keep status. Guarantees: `invalidate_child`/
   267	`invalidate_root` (`tree.py:117-144`), **latest-wins replan queue is NOT executed in
   268	the request** — the invalidate endpoint marks-invalidated + `submit_replan` (enqueue
   269	only) post-commit (`days/api.py:191-195`), and execution happens only when
   270	`process_pending_replan` runs, which in test mode is reachable ONLY via
   271	`POST /api/test/tick-boundary` (`scheduler.py:227-273`; no background loops in test
   272	mode, `server.py:92`). So every invalidate is followed by one tick-boundary.
   273	
   274	**Ordering (justified):** `replan_root` returns a tree with **zero children**
   275	(`fakes.py:70`). Do **child-invalidate first** (children still exist), then
   276	root-invalidate. Both halves live in this one `test_e29_` function.
   277	
   278	Setup (two carryover children):
   279	1. `a = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E29_A_TITLE)["id"]`;
   280	   `b = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E29_B_TITLE)["id"]`.
   281	2. `cli(server,"day","add-ticket",a,DAY_PREV)` then `cli(server,"day","add-ticket",b,DAY_PREV)`
   282	   → positions 0,1 on 2026-07-04 (`_read_yesterday_tickets` orders by position,
   283	   `boundary.py:106-116`).
   284	3. `_set_now(api, server, NOW_0501)`; `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert
   285	   `rep == {"planning_date":DAY_CUR,"ran":True,"judgment":"ok","replan":None}`.
   286	4. `page = open_page(ctx, server, "#/day", 'section[data-screen="day"]', settled=True)`;
   287	   `page.wait_for_selector('.plan-tree [data-node="1"]', timeout=WAIT_MS)`.
   288	   Assert initial: `[data-node="0"] .plan-node-note == E29_A_TITLE`,
   289	   `[data-node="1"] .plan-node-note == E29_B_TITLE`, both `data-status="proposed"`,
   290	   root focus `FAKE_FOCUS` `data-status="proposed"`.
   291	
   292	Child invalidate (node 0):
   293	5. `api.human_post(server, f"/api/day/{DAY_CUR}/plan/invalidate", {"node": 0})`
   294	   (`node` an **int**; the server rejects string positions, `days/api.py:83-90`).
   295	   Use `api.human_post` (blocks until committed+enqueued) so the following tick sees
   296	   the queued request — a UI click would leave an async gap where the tick could drain
   297	   an empty queue (race). `human_post` is header-less → human → `reject_agents` passes
   298	   (`days/api.py:181`).
   299	6. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)` drains the replan. Assert
   300	   `rep["ran"] is False` (2026-07-05 boundary already ran; idempotent guard
   301	   `boundary.py:44`), `rep["judgment"] == "ok"`, and
   302	   `rep["replan"] == {"attempts": [{"day_id": f"day_{DAY_CUR}", "scope": "child",
   303	   "node": 0, "outcome": "stored"}]}` (`scheduler.py:224`).
   304	7. `_wait_node_note(page, 0, FAKE_REPLAN_CHILD)` (WS-driven re-render). Then assert
   305	   "only that child replaced (others keep status/note)":
   306	   - `[data-node="0"] .plan-node-note == FAKE_REPLAN_CHILD`, `data-status="proposed"`
   307	     (replan_child returns proposed, position preserved, `fakes.py:78-83`).
   308	   - `[data-node="1"] .plan-node-note == E29_B_TITLE`, `data-status="proposed"` (unchanged).
   309	   - `[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus == FAKE_FOCUS`, `data-status="proposed"` (unchanged).
   310	
   311	Root invalidate:
   312	8. `api.human_post(server, f"/api/day/{DAY_CUR}/plan/invalidate", {"node": "root"})`
   313	   (string `"root"`). This marks root + every child invalidated and enqueues one
   314	   ReplanRoot (`tree.py:117-127`).
   315	9. `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert
   316	   `rep["replan"]["attempts"] == [{"day_id": f"day_{DAY_CUR}", "scope": "root",
   317	   "node": "root", "outcome": "stored"}]`.
   318	10. Wait for the replacement tree to render (zero children, `fakes.py:70`):

   320	    " .plan-node-focus'); return !!f && f.textContent === '" + FAKE_REPLAN_FOCUS + "'"
   321	    " && document.querySelectorAll('.plan-tree .plan-node--child').length === 0; }",
   322	    timeout=WAIT_MS)`. Then assert:
   323	    - `page.inner_text('[data-node="root"] .plan-node-focus') == FAKE_REPLAN_FOCUS`.

    63	def _set_now(api, server, iso):
    64	    return api.human_post(server, "/api/test/set-now", {"now": iso})

    75	def _read_claim(server, ticket_id):
    76	    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(server.db_path))
    77	    try:
    78	        row = conn.execute(
    79	            "SELECT claim_lock FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)
    80	        ).fetchone()
    81	    finally:
    82	        conn.close()
    83	    assert row is not None and row[0], row
    84	    return str(row[0])
    85	
    86	
    87	def _wait_node_note(page: Page, node, text):
    88	    page.wait_for_function(
    89	        "a => { const n = document.querySelector('[data-node=\"'+a.node+'\"] .plan-node-note');"
    90	        " return !!n && n.textContent === a.text; }",
    91	        arg={"node": str(node), "text": text},
    92	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
    93	    )
    94	
    95	
    96	def _grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, tid, ceiling, bodies):
    97	    # Human grant (header-less → human; grant_ticket rejects agents, tickets/api.py:359).
    98	    g = api.human_post(
    99	        server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}/grant", {"ceiling": ceiling, "at_cap": "propose"}
   100	    )
   101	    assert g["ceiling"] == ceiling and g["at_cap"] == "propose", g
   102	    # Claimless CLI proposals auto-accept up the chain to in_progress (like flows_a e27).
   103	    for field in ("success", "approach", "plan"):
   104	        cli(server, "propose", field, "--body-file", "-", ticket_id=tid, stdin=bodies[field])
   105	    d = api.get(server, f"/api/tickets/{tid}")
   106	    assert d["state"] == "in_progress", d
   107	    return d
   108

   110	def _reload_settle(page: Page, ready_selector):
   111	    # open_page opens FRESH pages; after page.reload() the __plannerDebug counters
   112	    # reset and the since=0 catch-up replay fires one flush — re-apply full discipline.
   113	    page.reload()
   114	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   115	    page.wait_for_function(
   116	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1",
   117	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
   118	    )
   119	    page.wait_for_function(
   120	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.flushes >= 1",
   121	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
   122	    )
   123	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)

   126	def _snap_ticket(p: Page):
   127	    return {
   128	        "state": p.get_attribute('section[data-screen="ticket"]', "data-state"),
   129	        "meta": p.inner_text('[data-field="result"] .proposal-card .proposal-meta'),
   130	        "body": p.inner_text('[data-field="result"] .proposal-card .markdown-block'),
   131	        "claim": p.eval_on_selector_all('[data-marker="running-claim"]', "e=>e.length"),
   132	        "run": p.eval_on_selector_all(
   133	            '[data-run-history] [data-run-row][data-run-status="running"]', "e=>e.length"
   134	        ),
   135	    }
   136

   142	        "pend": p.eval_on_selector_all(f'{card} [data-marker="pending-proposal"]', "e=>e.length"),
   143	        "claim": p.eval_on_selector_all(f'{card} [data-marker="running-claim"]', "e=>e.length"),
   144	    }

   438	        )

   440	        assert p.evaluate("window.__plannerDebug.flushes") > f0
   441	
   442	    cur = api.get(server, "/api/sprint/current")

   444	    assert iid not in [i["id"] for i in cur["groups"]["todo"]], cur

   377	### test_e31_refresh_restores_state(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api)
   378	
   379	Satisfies SPEC line 310: on Day, Board, and Ticket **mid-flow (pending proposal,
   380	running claim)** a reload renders identical content. Guarantees: stateless render / "refresh
   381	restores identical state" (SPEC §10 line 191, `app.js:1-5`), board markers §10.3
   382	(`board_view` `tickets/views.py:245-246`, `screens-board.js:34-49`), pending-proposal
   383	parked at ceiling with `at_cap=propose` (§4.3, `resolution.py:119-139`), running claim
   384	(dispatch).
   385	
   386	**Mid-flow construction** — one ticket carries BOTH a pending gating-field proposal
   387	AND a running claim. This requires claiming *before* the proposal is filed (a
   388	gating-pending ticket is dispatch-ineligible, `eligibility.py:53`), and a ceiling that
   389	makes the result proposal PARK rather than advance ⇒ **ceiling `in_progress`**
   390	(target `needs_review` > ceiling ⇒ no auto-accept, but `state==ceiling==in_progress`
   391	with `at_cap=propose` ⇒ dispatch-eligible branch (b), `eligibility.py:59`).
   392	
   393	1. `mid = cli(server,"ticket","create","--title",E31_TITLE)["id"]`.
   394	2. `_grant_and_advance(server, api, cli, mid, "in_progress")` with E31_SUCCESS/APPROACH/
   395	   PLAN → `in_progress`, ceiling `in_progress`, `at_cap propose`, no claim, no pending.
   396	3. Day plan (a "pending plan tree"): `_set_now(api, server, NOW_0501)`;
   397	   `rep = _tick_boundary(api, server)`; assert `rep == {"planning_date":DAY_CUR,
   398	   "ran":True,"judgment":"ok","replan":None}`. Carryover is empty (no 2026-07-04
   399	   day-tickets) ⇒ **root-only proposed tree** (`fakes.py:59`, children=[]).
   400	4. `rep = _tick_dispatcher(api, server)`; assert `len(rep["spawned"])==1` and
   401	   `rep["spawned"][0]["ticket_id"]==mid` (only `mid` is eligible). `mid` now has a
   402	   running claim + run.
   403	5. Worker files the result (claimless): `r = cli(server,"propose","result",
   404	   "--body-file","-",ticket_id=mid,stdin=E31_RESULT)`; assert `r["state"]=="in_progress"`
   405	   and `r["fields"]["result"]["proposal"]["body"]==E31_RESULT` (PARKED, not advanced).
   406	   Now: `in_progress` + pending `result` proposal + active claim = the mid-flow state.

   409	`before == after` AND the exact values.

   508	---
   509	
   510	## 6. Risks & race notes (each neutralized; wait-on-condition then assert)
   511	
   512	- **Replan is NOT synchronous (e29).** `POST …/plan/invalidate` only marks-invalidated
   513	  and *enqueues* (`days/api.py:191-195`); execution is in `process_pending_replan`,
   514	  reachable in test mode ONLY via `POST /api/test/tick-boundary` (`scheduler.py:265`;
   515	  no background loops, `server.py:92`). Neutralize: after every invalidate, issue one
   516	  `_tick_boundary` and assert its `replan.attempts[…].outcome == "stored"` BEFORE the
   517	  DOM wait. Issue the invalidate via `api.human_post` (blocks until commit+enqueue) so
   518	  the tick cannot drain an empty queue.
   519	- **e29 ordering hazard.** `replan_root` returns zero children (`fakes.py:70`), so
   520	  child-invalidate MUST precede root-invalidate — otherwise no child remains to
   521	  invalidate. Encoded in the step order.
   522	- **WS-flush timing (e28 accept-all, e30 run/flip, e32 status move).** Every observed
   523	  mutation is asserted only after `wait_for_selector`/`wait_for_function` on the
   524	  post-flush condition (rows present, `data-state`, status-group membership), never a
   525	  bare read; flush progress is additionally asserted via `window.__plannerDebug.flushes`
   526	  where "no reload" is the claim (e28, e32) — the flows_a pattern.
   527	- **`open_page` opens fresh pages; `page.reload()` does not (e31).** `_reload_settle`
   528	  re-applies ready-selector + `wsOpens>=1`, and each snapshot is taken only after its
   529	  mid-flow selector re-appears post-reload. The fixed test clock (never advanced past
   530	  05:01) keeps the 900s claim active, so `running-claim`/running-run markers persist
   531	  identically across the reload. Snapshots exclude timestamps (`run-history-times`,
   532	  `formatUnix`) — only statuses, notes, markers, bodies, and `data-state` are compared.
   533	- **Dispatcher claims exactly the intended ticket (e30, e31).** Each test's DB has a
   534	  single dispatch-eligible ticket at tick time, so `spawned` has length 1; asserted.
   535	  (In e31, ceiling `in_progress` + `at_cap propose` is what makes `mid` eligible at
   536	  `in_progress` via branch (b); after the result parks, it becomes ineligible but is
   537	  already claimed.)
   538	- **Claim-env unobtainable (e30) / shell gap (e32).** See §0. e30 uses the §7.6
   539	  claimless path and omits `plan run close`; e32 depends on the one-line `server.py`
   540	  shell edit. Both flagged for the integrator; the test bodies below assume the shell
   541	  edit is applied.
   542	- **`ran`/`judgment` report values.** First tick per date: `ran True`, `judgment "ok"`.
   543	  A second tick same date (e29 drains): `ran False`, `judgment "ok"` (idempotent guard,
   544	  `boundary.py:44`). Pinned in the report asserts.
   545	
   546	## 7. Fence-compliance checklist
   547	
   548	- Exactly one anchored test each: `test_e28_…`, `test_e29_…`, `test_e30_…`,
   549	  `test_e31_…`, `test_e32_…`. No other `test_`-prefixed names.
   550	- Helpers `_set_now`, `_tick_boundary`, `_tick_dispatcher`, `_wait_node_note`,
   551	  `_grant_and_advance`, `_reload_settle`, `_snap_ticket`, `_snap_board`, `_snap_day`
   552	  are all non-`test_`.
   553	- No `parametrize`, no `pytest.skip`/`xfail`/`.only`, no commented-out tests, no empty
   554	  bodies. Module docstring present (§3).
   555	- All asserted values are SPEC-exact literals (planning dates, fake-adapter strings,
   556	  node statuses, ticket states, report shapes), never weakened.
   557	```

   563	**A1 — Day ready selectors (codex F1, accepted).** There is no Day `<section>`:
   564	app.js renders every screen into a `div` (app.js:59-62) and screens-day.js:23 sets

   567	- e28 step 5: `open_page(..., "#/day", '.plan-tree [data-node="0"]', settled=True)`
   568	  (step 7's separate wait for `[data-node="0"]` becomes redundant; keep the assertions).

   571	Board (`section[data-screen="board"]`) and Ticket (`section[data-screen="ticket"]…`)
   572	selectors are correct as planned and unchanged.
   573	
   574	**A2 — `_reload_settle` full settled discipline (codex F2, accepted).** After
   575	`page.reload()` the `__plannerDebug` counters reset and the since=0 catch-up replay
   576	fires one flush; the "after" snapshot must not race it. Final helper:
   577	
   578	```python
   579	def _reload_settle(page, ready_selector):
   580	    page.reload()
   581	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   582	    page.wait_for_function(
   583	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.wsOpens >= 1",
   584	        timeout=WAIT_MS)
   585	    page.wait_for_function(
   586	        "() => window.__plannerDebug && window.__plannerDebug.flushes >= 1",
   587	        timeout=WAIT_MS)
   588	    page.wait_for_selector(ready_selector, timeout=WAIT_MS)
   589	```
   590	Callers still re-wait their mid-flow selector before taking the "after" snapshot.
   591	
   592	**A3 — e30 is SPEC-LITERAL: claim env + `plan run close` (replaces §0 finding 2 and
   593	every "claimless" step in the e30 blueprint; §0 finding 2's premise was wrong).**
   594	The claim token lives in `tickets.claim_lock` (dispatch/data.py:55) and the test owns
   595	its server's temp DB via `server.db_path` — read it there. The DB is WAL
   596	(core/db.py:152), so use a plain connection (not a `mode=ro` URI), one SELECT, close
   597	immediately:
   598	
   599	```python
   600	import sqlite3  # module top
   601	
   602	def _read_claim(server, ticket_id):
   603	    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(server.db_path))
   604	    try:
   605	        row = conn.execute(
   606	            "SELECT claim_lock FROM tickets WHERE id=?", (ticket_id,)).fetchone()
   607	    finally:
   608	        conn.close()
   609	    assert row is not None and row[0], row
   610	    return str(row[0])
   611	```
   612	
   613	e30 steps 6-7 become (steps 1-5 and 8-10 unchanged except as noted):
   614	6a. `token = _read_claim(server, mid)` (after the tick; the claim CAS has committed —
   615	    the tick report returned).
   616	6b. Worker proposes WITH the claim env (validated by `require_claim`,
   617	    tickets/api.py:296-297; the resolution engine never consults the claim, so the
   618	    auto-accept to `needs_review` is unchanged):

   620	    run_id=run_id, claim=token, stdin=E30_RESULT)`; assert `r["state"] == "needs_review"`.
   621	7.  Ticket page flips without reload (unchanged wait for `data-state == "needs_review"`).
   622	7b. Worker closes the run: `c = cli(server, "run", "close", "--outcome", "done",
   623	    run_id=run_id, claim=token)` (`require_claim` resolves run→ticket,
   624	    dispatch/api.py:49-56; claim still active — nothing between claim and close
   625	    cleared it). Assert `c["run"]["status"] == "done"` and
   626	    `c["run"]["id"] == run_id`. Ticket state is untouched by close ⇒ still parked
   627	    `needs_review`; cross-check `api.get(server, f"/api/tickets/{mid}")["state"] ==

   629	7c. `run_closed` is a logged event (dispatch/data.py:206) ⇒ flush-driven UI update:

    44	def _free_port() -> int:
    45	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
    46	        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
    47	        return int(sock.getsockname()[1])
    48	
    49	
    50	def _scrubbed_env() -> dict[str, str]:
    51	    """os.environ minus every ambient PLAN_* key (kills shell leakage)."""
    52	    return {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("PLAN_")}
    53	
    54	
    55	def _log_tail(log_path: Path, n: int = 40) -> str:
    56	    try:
    57	        lines = log_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
    58	    except OSError:
    59	        return "(no server log)"
    60	    return "\n".join(lines[-n:])

   102	                env=env,

   108	        handle = ServerHandle(base=base, proc=proc, db_path=db_path, log_path=log_path)
   109	        handles.append(handle)
   110	
   111	        deadline = time.time() + BOOT_BUDGET_S
   112	        while time.time() < deadline:
   113	            if proc.poll() is not None:
   114	                raise AssertionError(

   127	                assert assets.status_code == 200, assets.status_code
   128	                return handle
   129	            time.sleep(0.1)
   130	
   131	        proc.terminate()
   132	        raise AssertionError(
   133	            f"plan serve did not become ready within {BOOT_BUDGET_S}s\n"
   134	            f"--- server log tail ---\n{_log_tail(log_path)}"
   135	        )
   136	
   137	    yield make
   138	
   139	    for handle in handles:
   140	        handle.proc.terminate()
   141	        try:
   142	            handle.proc.wait(timeout=5)
   143	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   144	            handle.proc.kill()
   145	            handle.proc.wait()

   268	
   269	**Fences:**
   270	- Each item maps 1:1 to a named test whose name includes the item number (`test_a01_...`, `test_e22_...`).

   274	
   275	External boundaries (hermes spawn, boundary/replan agent, chat gateway) are faked in all tests. E2E uses a real server + temp SQLite DB + `PLAN_TEST_MODE=1`, driving ticks via the test endpoints and time via `PLAN_FAKE_NOW`. Multi-surface behavior uses two browser contexts.
   276	
   277	**Unit (pytest, pure logic + engine over a temp DB):**
   278	1. Planning-date math: 2026-07-05T04:59 local → planning date 2026-07-04; T05:00 → 2026-07-05; boundary hour honored from config.
   279	2. Gating chain: accepting success/approach/plan/result proposals advances exactly one state each, in order, with one `state_changed` event per step.
   280	3. Ceiling auto-accept: ceiling `needs_plan` with `at_cap = propose`, proposals filed on success then approach auto-accept and advance; the plan proposal stays pending (state `needs_plan`, ticket in approval queue).
   281	4. At-cap stop vs propose: at its ceiling with `at_cap = stop`, any agent proposal — including the current gating field — is rejected with the structured error; with `at_cap = propose`, a proposal on the current gating field files as pending and nothing advances; below the ceiling the behavior is identical for both values.
   282	5. One pending proposal per field: a second proposal supersedes the first; `proposal_superseded` event carries the replaced body.
   283	6. Edit-accept: accepting with edited text stores the edited text exactly as `value`, flags `edited: true` in the event.
   284	7. Result routing: accepted result with ceiling `needs_review` → state `needs_review`; with ceiling `done` → `done`; approve action in `needs_review` → `done`.
   285	8. Recap: write at `needs_success` rejected with structured error; write at `needs_approach` succeeds, overwrites prior recap, logs event, changes no state.
   286	9. Blocking: ticket blocked by an open ticket is dispatch-ineligible; blocker → `done` makes it eligible; `blocks` cycle creation rejected.
   287	10. Sprint-item permissions: agent `todo→active` succeeds; agent direct `active→done` write rejected; `done` via proposal + accept succeeds; `blocked_by` list stored and `blockers_cleared` computed when all blockers done.

   291	14. Claim CAS: two concurrent claim attempts on one ticket → exactly one wins, one `runs` row created.

     1	/* Screen 4: Ticket (#/ticket/<id>) — T16 (SPEC §10.4). Stateless render over five
     2	 * parallel fetches (detail + events + runs + sprints + chat status). Field
     3	 * sections show the value as markdown; a pending proposal composes T15's
     4	 * proposalCard (grant-pair picker gated on the gating field only); notes/recap are
     5	 * inline field editors. State/Grant controls, Copy, Links, Day/Sprint assignment,
     6	 * Run history, Event log, and the T15 chat panel round it out. Every mutation calls
     7	 * fetchJson and relies on the WS flush -> route() re-render; no optimistic UI. The
     8	 * lone direct DOM tweak is the copy button's "Copied" flash (a read, not a
     9	 * WS-mutating action, so no flush follows). Classic script: IIFE + "use strict",
    10	 * createElement only. */
    11	(function () {
    12	  "use strict";
    13	  var Planner = window.Planner;
    14	  var config = Planner.config;
    15	  var C = Planner.components;
    16	  var api = Planner.api;
    17	
    18	  var LINK_KINDS = ["belongs_to", "parent_child", "blocks", "relates"];
    19	  var FIELD_NAMES = ["success", "approach", "plan", "result"];
    20	  var COPY_FLASH_MS = 1500;
    21	
    22	  function el(tag, className, text) {
    23	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    24	    if (className) {
    25	      node.className = className;
    26	    }
    27	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    28	      node.textContent = text;
    29	    }
    30	    return node;
    31	  }
    32	
    33	  // Marker chip: value === variant, so "blocked" (no known chip variant) shows its
    34	  // own text via the default branch while known markers render their canned label.
    35	  function marker(v) {
    36	    var chip = C.chip(v, v);
    37	    chip.setAttribute("data-marker", v);
    38	    return chip;
    39	  }
    40	
    41	  // Plain-text fetch for copy-text: fetchJson would JSON.parse the text body and
    42	  // reject. The reject shape only needs .code/.message for errorLine.
    43	  function fetchText(path) {
    44	    return fetch(path).then(function (r) {
    45	      if (!r.ok) {
    46	        var err = new Error("HTTP " + r.status);
    47	        err.code = "http_error";
    48	        return Promise.reject(err);
    49	      }
    50	      return r.text();
    51	    });
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  function copyToClipboard(text) {
    55	    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    56	      return navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    57	    }
    58	    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    59	      var ta = el("textarea");
    60	      ta.value = text;
    61	      ta.setAttribute("readonly", "");
    62	      ta.style.position = "fixed";
    63	      ta.style.opacity = "0";
    64	      document.body.appendChild(ta);
    65	      ta.select();
    66	      var copied = false;
    67	      try {
    68	        copied = document.execCommand("copy");
    69	      } catch (e) {
    70	        copied = false;
    71	      }
    72	      document.body.removeChild(ta);
    73	      if (copied) {
    74	        resolve();
    75	      } else {
    76	        reject(new Error("copy failed"));
    77	      }
    78	    });
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  // In-flight discipline for the screen's own buttons (links/day/sprint/copy): clear
    82	  // any prior error, disable, run. On rejection re-enable and surface the error;
    83	  // returns the promise so copy can flash after a successful clipboard write. For a
    84	  // WS-mutating action the caller does NOT re-enable — the flush re-render replaces
    85	  // the DOM.
    86	  function submit(button, errorHost, fn) {
    87	    var prior = errorHost.querySelector(".error-line");
    88	    if (prior) {
    89	      errorHost.removeChild(prior);
    90	    }
    91	    button.disabled = true;
    92	    return Promise.resolve(fn()).then(
    93	      function (res) {
    94	        return res;
    95	      },
    96	      function (err) {
    97	        button.disabled = false;
    98	        errorHost.prepend(C.errorLine(err));
    99	        return Promise.reject(err);
   100	      }
   101	    );
   102	  }
   103	
   104	  function selectEl(options, selectedValue) {
   105	    var select = el("select", "form-control");
   106	    options.forEach(function (opt) {
   107	      var option = el("option", null, opt.label);
   108	      option.value = opt.value;
   109	      select.appendChild(option);
   110	    });
   111	    if (selectedValue !== undefined && selectedValue !== null) {
   112	      select.value = selectedValue;
   113	    }
   114	    return select;
   115	  }

   119	  function headerPanel(detail) {
   120	    var chips = el("div", "ticket-header-chips");
   121	    chips.appendChild(C.chip("state", detail.state));
   122	    chips.appendChild(C.chip("priority", detail.priority));
   123	    if (detail.project) {
   124	      chips.appendChild(C.chip("project", detail.project));
   125	    }
   126	    if (detail.deadline) {
   127	      chips.appendChild(C.chip("deadline", detail.deadline));
   128	    }
   129	    if (detail.claim_active) {
   130	      chips.appendChild(marker("running-claim"));
   131	    }
   132	    if (detail.auto_blocked) {
   133	      chips.appendChild(marker("auto-blocked"));
   134	    }
   135	    if (detail.blocked) {
   136	      chips.appendChild(marker("blocked"));
   137	    }
   138	    return C.panel(detail.title, [chips]);
   139	  }

   141	  function fieldPanel(id, detail, name) {
   142	    var slot = detail.fields[name];
   143	    var body = el("div", "ticket-field");
   144	    body.setAttribute("data-field", name);
   145	    body.appendChild(C.markdownBlock(slot.value));
   146

   148	      body.appendChild(C.proposalCard({
   149	        proposal: slot.proposal,
   150	        requireGrant: name === C.gatingField(detail.state),
   151	        newState: C.advanceTarget(detail.state, detail.ceiling),
   152	        onAccept: function (payload) {
   153	          return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/accept/" + name, {
   154	            method: "POST",
   155	            body: payload
   156	          });
   157	        }
   158	      }));
   159	    }
   160	
   161	    body.appendChild(C.fieldEditor(slot.notes, function (note) {
   162	      return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/notes/" + name, {
   163	        method: "PUT",
   164	        body: { note: note }
   165	      });
   166	    }));

   169	  }

   224	    button.type = "button";
   225	    button.setAttribute("data-copy", "");

   227	      submit(button, errorHost, function () {
   228	        return fetchText("/api/tickets/" + id + "/copy-text").then(copyToClipboard);
   229	      }).then(
   230	        function () {
   231	          // A read, not a WS-mutating action: no flush re-render follows, so the
   232	          // flash is the only feedback and is set directly (the sole sanctioned
   233	          // direct DOM tweak in this screen).

   237	            button.textContent = "Copy ticket";

   239	        },
   240	        function () {}
   241	      );
   242	    });

   244	    wrap.appendChild(button);

   249	  function endpointNode(endpointId, currentId) {
   250	    if (endpointId.indexOf("t_") === 0 && endpointId !== currentId) {
   251	      var anchor = el("a", "ticket-link-target", endpointId);
   252	      anchor.setAttribute("href", config.ROUTES.ticketPrefix + endpointId);
   253	      return anchor;
   254	    }
   255	    return el("span", "ticket-link-endpoint", endpointId);
   256	  }

   258	  function linkRow(id, link, errorHost) {
   259	    var row = el("div", "ticket-link-row");
   260	    row.setAttribute("data-link-row", "");
   261	    row.setAttribute("data-link-kind", link.kind);
   262	    row.appendChild(el("span", "ticket-link-kind", link.kind + ":"));
   263	    row.appendChild(endpointNode(link.from_id, id));
   264	    row.appendChild(el("span", "ticket-link-arrow", "→"));
   265	    row.appendChild(endpointNode(link.to_id, id));
   266	    var remove = el("button", "button", "Remove");
   267	    remove.type = "button";
   268	    remove.setAttribute("data-link-remove", "");
   269	    remove.addEventListener("click", function () {
   270	      submit(remove, errorHost, function () {
   271	        var qs = "from_id=" + encodeURIComponent(link.from_id) +
   272	          "&to_id=" + encodeURIComponent(link.to_id) +
   273	          "&kind=" + encodeURIComponent(link.kind);
   274	        return api.fetchJson("/api/links?" + qs, { method: "DELETE" });
   275	      });
   276	    });
   277	    row.appendChild(remove);
   278	    return row;
   279	  }

   289

   293	    toInput.setAttribute("placeholder", "to id");

   296	      LINK_KINDS.map(function (k) { return { value: k, label: k }; }),
   297	      LINK_KINDS[0]

   299	    kindSelect.setAttribute("data-link-kind-select", "");

   305	      submit(add, errorHost, function () {
   306	        return api.fetchJson("/api/links", {
   307	          method: "POST",

   309	            from_id: fromInput.value,

   311	            kind: kindSelect.value

   339	  function dayPanel(id, detail) {

   354	          return api.fetchJson(

   356	            { method: "DELETE" }

   358	        });
   359	      });

   361	      wrap.appendChild(row);
   362	    });

   368	    dateInput.value = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   369	    var add = el("button", "button", "Add to day");
   370	    add.type = "button";
   371	    add.setAttribute("data-day-add-btn", "");
   372	    add.addEventListener("click", function () {
   373	      submit(add, errorHost, function () {
   374	        return api.fetchJson("/api/day/" + dateInput.value + "/tickets", {
   375	          method: "POST",
   376	          body: { ticket_id: id }
   377	        });
   378	      });
   379	    });
   380	    addRow.appendChild(dateInput);
   381	    addRow.appendChild(add);
   382	    wrap.appendChild(addRow);

   387	  function sprintPanel(id, detail, sprints) {
   388	    var errorHost = el("div", "ticket-sprint-error");

   390	    wrap.setAttribute("data-sprint-assign", "");

   392	    if (detail.sprint_item_id !== null && detail.sprint_item_id !== undefined) {
   393	      // Parented: sprint is derived through the item; the server rejects set_sprint.
   394	      wrap.appendChild(el("div", "ticket-sprint-derived",
   395	        "sprint: " + (detail.effective_sprint_id || "(none)")));
   396	      return C.panel("Sprint", [wrap]);

   401	      options.push({ value: s.id, label: s.name });
   402	    });

   408	    save.setAttribute("data-sprint-save", "");
   409	    save.addEventListener("click", function () {
   410	      submit(save, errorHost, function () {
   411	        return api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id, {
   412	          method: "PATCH",
   413	          body: { sprint_id: select.value || null }
   414	        });
   415	      });
   416	    });
   417	
   418	    wrap.appendChild(select);
   419	    wrap.appendChild(save);
   420	    wrap.appendChild(errorHost);
   421	    return C.panel("Sprint", [wrap]);
   422	  }

   424	  // --- assembly --------------------------------------------------------------
   425	
   426	  function build(root, id, detail, evs, runs, sprintsRes, statusRes) {
   427	    var section = el("section", "ticket-screen");
   428	    section.setAttribute("data-screen", "ticket");
   429	    section.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", id);
   430	    section.setAttribute("data-state", detail.state);
   431	
   432	    section.appendChild(headerPanel(detail));
   433	    FIELD_NAMES.forEach(function (name) {
   434	      section.appendChild(fieldPanel(id, detail, name));
   435	    });
   436	    section.appendChild(recapPanel(id, detail));
   437	    section.appendChild(statePanel(id, detail));
   438	    section.appendChild(grantPanel(id, detail));
   439	    section.appendChild(copyPanel(id));
   440	    section.appendChild(linksPanel(id, detail));
   441	    section.appendChild(dayPanel(id, detail));
   442	    section.appendChild(sprintPanel(id, detail, sprintsRes.sprints || []));
   443	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Runs", [C.runHistory(runs.runs)]));
   444	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Events", [C.eventLog(evs.events)]));
   445	
   446	    var chatWrap = el("div", "ticket-chat");
   447	    chatWrap.setAttribute("data-chat", "");
   448	    chatWrap.appendChild(C.chatPanel(id, { available: statusRes.available }));
   449	    section.appendChild(C.panel("Chat", [chatWrap]));
   450	
   451	    root.appendChild(section);
   452	  }
   453	
   454	  function render(root, params) {
   455	    var id = params.id;
   456	    Promise.all([
   457	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id),
   458	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/events"),
   459	      api.fetchJson("/api/tickets/" + id + "/runs"),
   460	      api.fetchJson("/api/sprints"),
   461	      api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + id + "/status")
   462	    ]).then(
   463	      function (results) {
   464	        build(root, id, results[0], results[1], results[2], results[3], results[4]);
   465	      },
   466	      function (err) {
   467	        root.appendChild(C.errorLine(err));
   468	      }
   469	    );
   470	  }
   471	
   472	  Planner.registerScreen("ticket", render);
   473	})();

    51	        });

    55	          method: "POST",

    57	        });

    66	          method: "POST"

    72	  function dayRow(t, date, container) {
    73	    var row = el("div", "day-ticket-row");
    74	    row.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", t.id);
    75	    var chips = [c.chip("priority", t.priority), c.chip("state", t.state)];
    76	    if (t.claim_active) {
    77	      chips.push(c.chip("running-claim"));
    78	    }
    79	    var gf = c.gatingField(t.state);
    80	    if (gf && t.fields[gf] && t.fields[gf].proposal) {
    81	      chips.push(c.chip("pending-proposal"));
    82	    }
    83	    row.appendChild(c.entityRow({ title: t.title, href: "#/ticket/" + t.id, chips: chips }));
    84	    // The remove button is a SIBLING of the entity-row anchor (never nested).
    85	    // Remove = defer (association-only delete, days/data.py:139-162).
    86	    var remove = el("button", "button", "Remove");
    87	    remove.type = "button";
    88	    remove.setAttribute("data-remove", "");
    89	    remove.addEventListener("click", function () {
    90	      var prior = container.querySelector(".error-line");
    91	      if (prior) {
    92	        container.removeChild(prior);
    93	      }
    94	      remove.disabled = true;
    95	      Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/day/" + date + "/tickets/" + t.id, { method: "DELETE" }).then(
    96	        function () {},   // A3: stay disabled on success; the flush re-render replaces the row
    97	        function (err) {
    98	          remove.disabled = false;
    99	          container.appendChild(c.errorLine(err));
   100	        }
   101	      );
   102	    });
   103	    row.appendChild(remove);
   104	    return row;
   105	  }

   107	  function build(root, day, queues, available) {

    20	  function appendChildren(parent, children) {
    21	    if (children === null || children === undefined) {
    22	      return;
    23	    }
    24	    if (Array.isArray(children)) {
    25	      children.forEach(function (child) {
    26	        if (child) {
    27	          parent.appendChild(child);
    28	        }
    29	      });
    30	    } else {
    31	      parent.appendChild(children);
    32	    }
    33	  }

    35	  // 5.1 App shell — the one component returning a handle, not a bare node.
    36	  function appShell() {
    37	    var config = Planner.config;
    38	    var shell = make("div", "shell");
    39	    var nav = make("header", "shell-nav");
    40	    var links = make("nav", "shell-links");
    41	    var badge = make("span", "nav-badge hidden");
    42	    var linkEls = {};
    43	    var items = [
    44	      ["day", "Day"],
    45	      ["review", "Review"],
    46	      ["board", "Board"],
    47	      ["sprint", "Sprint"],
    48	      ["backlog", "Backlog"]
    49	    ];
    50	    items.forEach(function (pair) {
    51	      var name = pair[0];
    52	      var anchor = make("a", "nav-link");
    53	      anchor.setAttribute("data-screen", name);
    54	      anchor.setAttribute("href", config.ROUTES[name]);
    55	      anchor.appendChild(document.createTextNode(pair[1]));
    56	      if (name === "review") {
    57	        anchor.appendChild(badge);
    58	      }
    59	      linkEls[name] = anchor;
    60	      links.appendChild(anchor);
    61	    });
    62	    nav.appendChild(make("span", "shell-brand", "planner"));
    63	    nav.appendChild(links);
    64	    var content = make("main", "shell-content");
    65	    shell.appendChild(nav);
    66	    shell.appendChild(content);
    67	
    68	    function setReviewBadge(n) {
    69	      if (n > 0) {
    70	        badge.textContent = String(n);
    71	        badge.classList.remove("hidden");
    72	      } else {
    73	        badge.classList.add("hidden");
    74	      }
    75	    }
    76	    function setActiveNav(name) {
    77	      items.forEach(function (pair) {
    78	        linkEls[pair[0]].classList.toggle("active", pair[0] === name);
    79	      });
    80	    }
    81	    // The shell IS the returned DOM node (ticket: components return DOM nodes);
    82	    // the router's handles ride on it as properties.
    83	    shell.content = content;
    84	    shell.setReviewBadge = setReviewBadge;
    85	    shell.setActiveNav = setActiveNav;
    86	    return shell;
    87	  }

    89	  // 5.2 Panel — the ONLY box primitive.
    90	  function panel(title, children) {
    91	    var section = make("section", "panel");
    92	    section.appendChild(make("h2", "panel-title", title));
    93	    var body = make("div", "panel-body");
    94	    appendChildren(body, children);
    95	    section.appendChild(body);
    96	    return section;
    97	  }

    99	  // 5.3 Markdown block.
   100	  function markdownBlock(text) {
   101	    if (!text || !String(text).trim()) {
   102	      return make("div", "quiet-line", "(none)");
   103	    }
   104	    var block = Planner.markdown.render(text);
   105	    block.classList.add("markdown-block");
   106	    return block;
   107	  }
   108	
   109	  // 5.4 Field editor — no optimistic UI; a successful save is reflected by the
   110	  // WS-invalidation refetch.
   111	  function fieldEditor(value, onSave) {
   112	    var wrap = make("div", "field-editor");
   113	    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input");
   114	    textarea.rows = 8;
   115	    textarea.value = value || "";
   116	    var actions = make("div", "field-editor-actions");
   117	    var button = make("button", "button button--primary", "Save");
   118	    button.type = "button";
   119	    button.addEventListener("click", function () {
   120	      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");
   121	      if (prior) {
   122	        actions.removeChild(prior);
   123	      }
   124	      button.disabled = true;
   125	      Promise.resolve(onSave(textarea.value)).then(
   126	        function () {},
   127	        function (err) {
   128	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   129	        }
   130	      ).then(function () {
   131	        button.disabled = false;
   132	      });
   133	    });
   134	    actions.appendChild(button);
   135	    wrap.appendChild(textarea);
   136	    wrap.appendChild(actions);
   137	    return wrap;
   138	  }

   140	  // 5.5 Meta chips — ONE component, variants. The accent appears exactly where a
   141	  // human is needed; everything else stays achromatic.
   142	  var CHIP_MARKERS = {
   143	    "pending-proposal": "proposal pending",
   144	    "running-claim": "● running",
   145	    "blockers-cleared": "blockers cleared",
   146	    "auto-blocked": "auto-blocked",
   147	    "frozen": "frozen"
   148	  };
   149	
   150	  function chip(variant, value, opts) {
   151	    opts = opts || {};
   152	    var span = make("span", "chip");
   153	    if (variant === "priority") {
   154	      span.classList.add("chip--priority");
   155	      span.classList.add("chip--" + String(value).toLowerCase());
   156	      span.textContent = String(value);
   157	    } else if (variant === "state") {
   158	      span.classList.add("chip--state");
   159	      span.setAttribute("data-value", String(value));
   160	      span.textContent = String(value).replace(/_/g, " ");
   161	    } else if (variant === "project") {
   162	      span.classList.add("chip--project");
   163	      span.textContent = String(value);
   164	    } else if (variant === "deadline") {
   165	      span.classList.add("chip--deadline");
   166	      if (opts.overdue) {
   167	        span.classList.add("chip--overdue");
   168	      }
   169	      span.textContent = String(value);
   170	    } else if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(CHIP_MARKERS, variant)) {
   171	      span.classList.add("chip--" + variant);
   172	      span.textContent = CHIP_MARKERS[variant];
   173	    } else {
   174	      span.textContent = value === undefined || value === null ? "" : String(value);
   175	    }
   176	    return span;
   177	  }
   178	
   179	  // 5.6 Entity row — a native anchor; state lives in the URL only.
   180	  function entityRow(opts) {
   181	    opts = opts || {};
   182	    var anchor = make("a", "entity-row");
   183	    anchor.setAttribute("href", opts.href || "#");
   184	    anchor.appendChild(make("span", "entity-row-title", opts.title || ""));
   185	    var chips = make("span", "entity-row-chips");
   186	    appendChildren(chips, opts.chips || null);
   187	    anchor.appendChild(chips);
   188	    return anchor;
   189	  }

   191	  // 5.7 Error line — consumes exactly what fetchJson rejections carry.
   192	  function errorLine(err) {
   193	    var line = make("div", "error-line");
   194	    line.appendChild(make("span", "error-code", (err && err.code) || "error"));
   195	    line.appendChild(make("span", "error-message", (err && err.message) || "request failed"));
   196	    return line;
   197	  }

     1	/* Screen 3: Board (#/board) — T16 (SPEC §10.3). Stateless render over /api/board:
     2	 * one column per ticket state (server order; `dropped` never present), cards are
     3	 * entity-row anchors decorated with data-* for e2e. The whole card is the
     4	 * click-through; no drag-and-drop, no client-side date math (the server sends no
     5	 * overdue flag). Registers the real "board" screen over app.js's placeholder
     6	 * (overwrite-wins). Classic script: IIFE + "use strict", createElement only. */
     7	(function () {
     8	  "use strict";
     9	  var Planner = window.Planner;
    10	  var config = Planner.config;
    11	  var C = Planner.components;
    12	  var api = Planner.api;
    13	
    14	  function el(tag, className, text) {
    15	    var node = document.createElement(tag);
    16	    if (className) {
    17	      node.className = className;
    18	    }
    19	    if (text !== undefined && text !== null) {
    20	      node.textContent = text;
    21	    }
    22	    return node;
    23	  }
    24	
    25	  // Marker chip reused from the chip primitive; value === variant so the "blocked"
    26	  // default branch (no known variant) still shows its own text, while the known
    27	  // marker variants ignore the value and render their canned label.
    28	  function marker(v) {
    29	    var chip = C.chip(v, v);
    30	    chip.setAttribute("data-marker", v);
    31	    return chip;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  function cardAnchor(card) {
    35	    var chips = [
    36	      C.chip("priority", card.priority),
    37	      card.deadline ? C.chip("deadline", card.deadline) : null,
    38	      card.project ? C.chip("project", card.project) : null,
    39	      card.has_pending_proposal ? marker("pending-proposal") : null,
    40	      card.has_running_claim ? marker("running-claim") : null
    41	    ];
    42	    var anchor = C.entityRow({
    43	      href: config.ROUTES.ticketPrefix + card.id,
    44	      title: card.title,
    45	      chips: chips
    46	    });
    47	    anchor.setAttribute("data-card", "");
    48	    anchor.setAttribute("data-ticket-id", card.id);
    49	    return anchor;
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  function render(root) {
    53	    api.fetchJson("/api/board").then(
    54	      function (board) {
    55	        var section = el("section", "board-screen");
    56	        section.setAttribute("data-screen", "board");
    57	        var lane = el("div", "board");
    58	        (board.columns || []).forEach(function (col) {
    59	          var column = el("div", "board-column");
    60	          column.setAttribute("data-column", col.state);
    61	
    62	          var head = el("div", "board-column-head");
    63	          head.appendChild(el("span", "board-column-name", col.state.replace(/_/g, " ")));
    64	          head.appendChild(el("span", "board-column-count", String(col.cards.length)));
    65	          column.appendChild(head);
    66	
    67	          var cards = el("div", "board-column-cards");
    68	          if (col.cards.length) {
    69	            col.cards.forEach(function (card) {
    70	              cards.appendChild(cardAnchor(card));
    71	            });
    72	          } else {
    73	            cards.appendChild(C.quietLine("(empty)"));
    74	          }
    75	          column.appendChild(cards);
    76	          lane.appendChild(column);
    77	        });
    78	        section.appendChild(lane);
    79	        root.appendChild(section);
    80	      },
    81	      function (err) {
    82	        root.appendChild(C.errorLine(err));
    83	      }
    84	    );
    85	  }
    86	
    87	  Planner.registerScreen("board", render);
    88	})();

   489	  // requireGrant, Accept is unfireable until the picker yields both halves.
   490	  function proposalCard(opts) {
   491	    var card = make("div", "proposal-card");
   492	    card.appendChild(make("div", "proposal-meta", "proposed by " + opts.proposal.proposed_by));
   493	    card.appendChild(markdownBlock(opts.proposal.body));
   494	    var textarea = make("textarea", "field-editor-input proposal-edit");
   495	    textarea.setAttribute("data-edit", "");

   500	    var picker = null;

   502	    var resolved = false;
   503	    var actions = make("div", "proposal-card-actions");

   505	    accept.type = "button";
   506	    accept.setAttribute("data-accept", "");
   507	
   508	    function sync() {
   509	      accept.disabled = inFlight || resolved ||
   510	        (opts.requireGrant && (picker === null || picker.getGrant() === null));
   511	    }
   512	
   513	    if (opts.requireGrant) {
   514	      picker = grantPairPicker(opts.newState, sync);
   515	      card.appendChild(picker);
   516	    }

   519	      var prior = actions.querySelector(".error-line");

   521	        actions.removeChild(prior);
   522	      }
   523	      var payload = {};
   524	      if (textarea.value !== opts.proposal.body) {
   525	        payload.edited_body = textarea.value;
   526	      }
   527	      if (opts.requireGrant) {
   528	        var grant = picker.getGrant();
   529	        payload.next_ceiling = grant.next_ceiling;
   530	        payload.at_cap = grant.at_cap;
   531	      }
   532	      inFlight = true;
   533	      sync();
   534	      Promise.resolve(opts.onAccept(payload)).then(
   535	        function () {
   536	          resolved = true;   // A3: stay disabled after a successful accept
   537	          inFlight = false;
   538	          sync();
   539	        },
   540	        function (err) {
   541	          inFlight = false;
   542	          actions.prepend(errorLine(err));
   543	          sync();
   544	        }
   545	      );
   546	    });
   547	
   548	    actions.appendChild(accept);
   549	    card.appendChild(actions);
   550	    sync();   // Accept starts disabled when a grant is required (nothing picked)
   551	    return card;
   552	  }

   619	  // --- D11 item 10: chat panel -----------------------------------------------
   620	  // entityId is the SERVER-provided chat id (day.id / ticket.id), never built
   621	  // client-side. Rebuilds its message list from the module-scope transcript, which
   622	  // the WS flush does not touch — that is the survival mechanism.
   623	  function chatPanel(entityId, opts) {
   624	    var panelEl = make("div", "chat-panel");
   625	    panelEl.setAttribute("data-chat-panel", "");
   626	    var messages = make("div", "chat-messages");
   627	    messages.setAttribute("data-chat-messages", "");
   628	    panelEl.appendChild(messages);
   629	
   630	    function paint() {
   631	      messages.replaceChildren();
   632	      (chatTranscripts[entityId] || []).forEach(function (msg) {
   633	        if (msg.who === "you") {
   634	          var you = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--you", msg.text);
   635	          you.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "you");
   636	          messages.appendChild(you);
   637	        } else {
   638	          var reply = make("div", "chat-msg chat-msg--planner");
   639	          reply.setAttribute("data-chat-msg", "planner");
   640	          reply.appendChild(markdownBlock(msg.text));
   641	          messages.appendChild(reply);
   642	        }
   643	      });
   644	    }
   645	    paint();
   646	
   647	    if (!opts.available) {
   648	      var offline = make("div", "chat-offline", "gateway offline");
   649	      offline.setAttribute("data-chat-offline", "");
   650	      panelEl.appendChild(offline);
   651	      return panelEl;   // §11 notice; no input source rendered when offline
   652	    }
   653	
   654	    var source;
   655	    var ctx = {
   656	      initialText: chatDrafts[entityId] || "",
   657	      onInput: function (text) {
   658	        chatDrafts[entityId] = text;
   659	      },
   660	      submit: function (text) {
   661	        if (!chatTranscripts[entityId]) {
   662	          chatTranscripts[entityId] = [];
   663	        }
   664	        chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "you", text: text });
   665	        delete chatDrafts[entityId];
   666	        paint();
   667	        return Planner.api.fetchJson("/api/chat/" + entityId + "/send", {
   668	          method: "POST",
   669	          body: { text: text }
   670	        }).then(
   671	          function (res) {
   672	            chatTranscripts[entityId].push({ who: "planner", text: res.reply_text });
   673	            if (panelEl.isConnected) {   // a flush may have replaced the panel mid-flight
   674	              paint();
   675	            }
   676	            return res;
   677	          },
   678	          function (err) {
   679	            var prior = panelEl.querySelector(".error-line");
   680	            if (prior) {
   681	              panelEl.removeChild(prior);
   682	            }
   683	            panelEl.insertBefore(errorLine(err), source);
   684	            return Promise.reject(err);
   685	          }
   686	        );
   687	      }
   688	    };
   689	    source = Planner.chatInput.sources[Planner.chatInput.active](ctx);
   690	    panelEl.appendChild(source);
   691	    return panelEl;
   692	  }

   704	    var head = make("div", "review-card-head");

   744	        return opts.onAcceptStatus();

   746	    }
   747

   776	  // ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   777	  // T16 additions (D11 items 11, 12, 14, 15) — State control, Grant control,
   778	  // Event log, Run history. Additive: nothing above this line is touched. These
   779	  // reuse the T15 grant-pair picker and the shared bindMutating discipline; the
   780	  // server re-validates every write, so the pickers only choose what to OFFER.
