     1	"""§4.4 resolution semantics as pure Decisions. _accept_gating_proposal is the
     2	sole constructor of any Decision that traverses the gating-acceptance edges 1-5
     3	(auto and human alike); _state_change is the sole constructor of state_changed
     4	EventSpecs. Together with data._apply_decision (the sole appender) this is the
     5	"one canonical writer per transition" guarantee (§4.4.6)."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Final
    10	
    11	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    12	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    13	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    14	    AtCap,
    15	    FieldName,
    16	    FieldSlot,
    17	    GrantPair,
    18	    NextCeiling,
    19	    Proposal,
    20	    Ticket,
    21	    TicketState,
    22	)
    23	from planner.tickets.logic import admission, fields_codec, machine
    24	from planner.tickets.logic.decisions import Decision, EventSpec
    25	
    26	CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "auto_accept"
    27	CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT: Final[str] = "human_accept"
    28	CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE: Final[str] = "review_approve"
    29	CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP: Final[str] = "human_state_jump"
    30	CAUSE_DROP: Final[str] = "drop"
    31	CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT: Final[str] = "onward_grant"
    32	CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT: Final[str] = "human_grant"
    33	RESOLVED_BY_AUTO: Final[str] = "auto"
    34	RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN: Final[str] = "human"
    35	
    36	
    37	def _state_change(old: TicketState, new: TicketState, cause: str) -> EventSpec:
    38	    return EventSpec(EventKind.state_changed, {"from": old.value, "to": new.value, "cause": cause})
    39	
    40	
    41	def _accept_gating_proposal(
    42	    ticket: Ticket,
    43	    field: FieldName,
    44	    stored_body: str,
    45	    resolved_by: str,
    46	    edited: bool,
    47	    grant: GrantPair | None,
    48	    cause: str,
    49	    superseded_body: str | None = None,
    50	) -> Decision:
    51	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
    52	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
    53	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
    54	    new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
    55	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
    56	    if superseded_body is not None:
    57	        events.append(
    58	            EventSpec(
    59	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
    60	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
    61	            )
    62	        )
    63	    events.append(
    64	        EventSpec(
    65	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
    66	            {
    67	                "field": field.value,
    68	                "body": stored_body,
    69	                "resolved_by": resolved_by,
    70	                "edited": edited,
    71	            },
    72	        )
    73	    )
    74	    events.append(_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, cause))
    75	    new_ceiling: TicketState | None = None
    76	    new_at_cap: AtCap | None = None
    77	    if grant is not None:
    78	        ceiling = grant.next_ceiling
    79	        assert isinstance(ceiling, TicketState)  # resolve_grant always yields a concrete state
    80	        new_ceiling = ceiling
    81	        new_at_cap = grant.at_cap
    82	        events.append(
    83	            EventSpec(
    84	                EventKind.grant_changed,
    85	                {
    86	                    "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    87	                    "at_cap": grant.at_cap.value,
    88	                    "cause": CAUSE_ONWARD_GRANT,
    89	                },
    90	            )
    91	        )
    92	    return Decision(
    93	        events=tuple(events),
    94	        new_fields=new_fields,
    95	        new_state=new_state,
    96	        new_ceiling=new_ceiling,
    97	        new_at_cap=new_at_cap,
    98	    )
    99	
   100	
   101	def decide_file_proposal(
   102	    ticket: Ticket, field: FieldName, body: str, actor: str, now: int
   103	) -> Decision:
   104	    admission.validate_body(body, "proposal body")
   105	    admission.check_agent_proposal(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, ticket.at_cap, field)
   106	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   107	    superseded_body = slot.proposal.body if slot.proposal is not None else None
   108	    if machine.auto_accept_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling, field) is not None:
   109	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   110	            ticket,
   111	            field,
   112	            stored_body=body,
   113	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_AUTO,
   114	            edited=False,
   115	            grant=None,
   116	            cause=CAUSE_AUTO_ACCEPT,
   117	            superseded_body=superseded_body,
   118	        )
   119	    new_slot = FieldSlot(
   120	        value=slot.value,
   121	        proposal=Proposal(body=body, proposed_by=actor, created_at=now),
   122	        notes=slot.notes,
   123	    )
   124	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   125	    events: list[EventSpec] = []
   126	    if superseded_body is not None:
   127	        events.append(
   128	            EventSpec(
   129	                EventKind.proposal_superseded,
   130	                {"field": field.value, "replaced_body": superseded_body},
   131	            )
   132	        )
   133	    events.append(
   134	        EventSpec(
   135	            EventKind.proposal_filed,
   136	            {"field": field.value, "body": body, "proposed_by": actor},
   137	        )
   138	    )
   139	    return Decision(events=tuple(events), new_fields=new_fields)
   140	
   141	
   142	def decide_accept(
   143	    ticket: Ticket,
   144	    field: FieldName,
   145	    actor: str,
   146	    edited_body: str | None,
   147	    next_ceiling: NextCeiling | None,
   148	    at_cap: AtCap | None,
   149	) -> Decision:
   150	    admission.require_human(actor, "accept_proposal")
   151	    if edited_body is not None:
   152	        admission.validate_body(edited_body, "edit-accept text")
   153	    edited = edited_body is not None
   154	    slot = fields_codec.get_slot(ticket.fields, field)
   155	    if slot.proposal is None:
   156	        raise PlannerError(
   157	            ErrorCode.not_found,
   158	            "no pending proposal on field",
   159	            {"ticket_id": ticket.id, "field": field.value},
   160	        )
   161	    stored_body = edited_body if edited_body is not None else slot.proposal.body
   162	    if field is machine.gating_field(ticket.state):
   163	        new_state = machine.advance_target(ticket.state, ticket.ceiling)
   164	        grant = machine.resolve_grant(new_state, next_ceiling, at_cap)
   165	        return _accept_gating_proposal(
   166	            ticket,
   167	            field,
   168	            stored_body=stored_body,
   169	            resolved_by=RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   170	            edited=edited,
   171	            grant=grant,
   172	            cause=CAUSE_HUMAN_ACCEPT,
   173	        )
   174	    new_slot = FieldSlot(value=stored_body, proposal=None, notes=slot.notes)
   175	    new_fields = fields_codec.with_slot(ticket.fields, field, new_slot)
   176	    events = (
   177	        EventSpec(
   178	            EventKind.proposal_accepted,
   179	            {
   180	                "field": field.value,
   181	                "body": stored_body,
   182	                "resolved_by": RESOLVED_BY_HUMAN,
   183	                "edited": edited,
   184	            },
   185	        ),
   186	    )
   187	    return Decision(events=events, new_fields=new_fields)
   188	
   189	
   190	def decide_approve(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   191	    admission.require_human(actor, "approve_review")
   192	    if ticket.state is not TicketState.needs_review:
   193	        raise PlannerError(
   194	            ErrorCode.validation,
   195	            "approve requires state needs_review",
   196	            {"state": ticket.state.value},
   197	        )
   198	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.done, CAUSE_REVIEW_APPROVE),)
   199	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.done)
   200	
   201	
   202	def decide_state_jump(ticket: Ticket, new_state: TicketState, actor: str) -> Decision:
   203	    admission.require_human(actor, "set_state")
   204	    if new_state is TicketState.dropped:
   205	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "use the drop action")
   206	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   207	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "dropped is terminal")
   208	    if new_state is ticket.state:
   209	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "ticket already in that state")
   210	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, new_state, CAUSE_HUMAN_STATE_JUMP),)
   211	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=new_state)
   212	
   213	
   214	def decide_drop(ticket: Ticket, actor: str) -> Decision:
   215	    admission.require_human(actor, "drop_ticket")
   216	    if ticket.state is TicketState.done:
   217	        raise PlannerError(
   218	            ErrorCode.validation, "done tickets cannot be dropped", {"state": "done"}
   219	        )
   220	    if ticket.state is TicketState.dropped:
   221	        raise PlannerError(
   222	            ErrorCode.validation, "ticket is already dropped", {"state": "dropped"}
   223	        )
   224	    events = (_state_change(ticket.state, TicketState.dropped, CAUSE_DROP),)
   225	    return Decision(events=events, new_state=TicketState.dropped)
   226	
   227	
   228	def decide_grant_change(
   229	    ticket: Ticket, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, actor: str
   230	) -> Decision:
   231	    admission.require_human(actor, "change_grant")
   232	    machine.validate_ceiling(ceiling)
   233	    events = (
   234	        EventSpec(
   235	            EventKind.grant_changed,
   236	            {"ceiling": ceiling.value, "at_cap": at_cap.value, "cause": CAUSE_HUMAN_GRANT},
   237	        ),
   238	    )
   239	    return Decision(events=events, new_ceiling=ceiling, new_at_cap=at_cap)

   168	def get_effective_sprint_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str | None:
   169	    ticket = _load_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   170	    if ticket.sprint_item_id is None:
   171	        return ticket.sprint_id
   172	    row = conn.execute(
   173	        "SELECT sprint_id FROM sprint_items WHERE id = ?", (ticket.sprint_item_id,)
   174	    ).fetchone()
   175	    if row is None:
   176	        return None
   177	    sprint_id: str | None = row["sprint_id"]
   178	    return sprint_id

   159	def _texts(page: Page, selector: str) -> list[str]:
   160	    """textContent of every match — exact, so title equality holds (chips are siblings)."""
   161	    return page.eval_on_selector_all(selector, "els => els.map(e => e.textContent)")

   164	def _wait_count(page: Page, selector: str, n: int) -> None:
   165	    page.wait_for_function(
   166	        "a => document.querySelectorAll(a.sel).length === a.n",
   167	        arg={"sel": selector, "n": n},
   168	        timeout=WAIT_MS,
   169	    )

   172	def test_e33_seed_fixture_ui(server_factory, context_factory, open_page, cli):
   173	    # Boot inside the fixture's sprint window (2026-06-08 -> 2026-06-21): 12:00 > 05:00
   174	    # boundary hour -> planning date 2026-06-11, so the Sprint screen resolves a current
   175	    # sprint instead of "No current sprint.".
   176	    server = server_factory(fake_now="2026-06-11T12:00:00")
   177	    report = cli(server, "seed", "--source", FIXTURE_SRC)
   178	    assert _report_counts(report) == (1, 6, 3, 4, 3, 1, 0)
   179	
   180	    ctx = context_factory()
   181	
   182	    # Board — seed events preceded page-open, so settled=True; the board section only
   183	    # exists after the /api/board fetch resolves.
   184	    page = open_page(ctx, server, "#/board", 'section[data-screen="board"]', settled=True)
   185	    for state, titles in BOARD_33:
   186	        assert _texts(page, f'[data-column="{state}"] [data-card] .entity-row-title') == titles
   187	
   188	    # Sprint — ready selector is the post-fetch header; its presence also proves the
   189	    # fake_now override worked ("No current sprint." never rendered).
   190	    page = open_page(
   191	        ctx, server, "#/sprint", '[data-screen="sprint"] .sprint-header', settled=True
   192	    )
   193	    assert _texts(page, ".sprint-header .screen-title") == ["Sprint 2026-06-08 to 2026-06-21"]
   194	    assert _texts(page, ".sprint-header .sprint-dates") == ["2026-06-08 – 2026-06-21"]
   195	    for status, titles in SPRINT_GROUPS_33:
   196	        assert (
   197	            _texts(page, f'[data-status-group="{status}"] [data-item-id] .entity-row-title')
   198	            == titles
   199	        )
   200	    loose = _texts(page, '[data-loose] [data-ticket-id] .entity-row-title')
   201	    assert len(loose) == 3
   202	    assert set(loose) == LOOSE_33
   203	
   204	    # Backlog — ready selector anchors a landed backlog-item row; the ideas list is a
   205	    # second independent fetch, so gate its row count before reading.
   206	    page = open_page(
   207	        ctx,
   208	        server,
   209	        "#/backlog",
   210	        '[data-screen="backlog"] [data-backlog-items] [data-item-id]',
   211	        settled=True,
   212	    )
   213	    _wait_count(page, "[data-ideas] [data-idea-id]", 3)

   227	def test_e34_snapshot_migration(server, context_factory, open_page, cli, api):
   228	    # Run 1 — report audit.

   242	
   243	    # API audit — tickets (list view carries alias + chat_session_key).

     6

    10	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import AtCap, FieldName, TicketState

    14	HUMAN_ACTOR: Final[str] = "human"
    15	
    16	
    17	def is_human(actor: str) -> bool:
    18	    return actor == HUMAN_ACTOR
    19	
    20	
    21	def require_human(actor: str, action: str) -> None:
    22	    if actor != HUMAN_ACTOR:
    23	        raise PlannerError(
    24	            ErrorCode.agent_forbidden,
    25	            f"{action} is a human-only action",
    26	            {"action": action, "actor": actor},
    27	        )
    28	
    29	
    30	def check_agent_proposal(
    31	    state: TicketState, ceiling: TicketState, at_cap: AtCap, field: FieldName
    32	) -> None:
    33	    if machine.is_terminal(state):
    34	        raise PlannerError(
    35	            ErrorCode.validation, "no proposals on a terminal ticket", {"state": state.value}
    36	        )
    37	    gating = machine.gating_field(state)
    38	    if not machine.at_or_beyond_ceiling(state, ceiling):
    39	        return
    40	    if at_cap is AtCap.stop:
    41	        raise PlannerError(
    42	            ErrorCode.at_cap_stop,
    43	            "ticket is at its ceiling with at_cap=stop",
    44	            {
    45	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    46	                "state": state.value,
    47	                "ceiling": ceiling.value,
    48	                "at_cap": "stop",
    49	            },
    50	        )
    51	    if field is not gating:
    52	        raise PlannerError(
    53	            ErrorCode.validation,
    54	            "at the ceiling agents may propose only the current gating field",
    55	            {
    56	                "field": field.value,
    57	                "gating_field": gating.value if gating is not None else None,
    58	                "state": state.value,
    59	            },
    60	        )
    61	
    62	
    63	def check_recap_writable(state: TicketState) -> None:
    64	    if state is TicketState.needs_success or state is TicketState.dropped:
    65	        raise PlannerError(
    66	            ErrorCode.recap_too_early,
    67	            "recap is writable only past needs_success",
    68	            {"state": state.value},
    69	        )
    70	
    71	
    72	def check_sprint_assignable(ticket_id: str, sprint_item_id: str | None) -> None:
    73	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
    74	        raise PlannerError(
    75	            ErrorCode.sprint_derived,
    76	            "sprint_id is derived from the parent item",
    77	            {"ticket_id": ticket_id, "sprint_item_id": sprint_item_id},
    78	        )
    79	
    80	
    81	def validate_title(title: str, max_chars: int) -> None:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "title must be non-empty")
    84	    if len(title) > max_chars:
    85	        raise PlannerError(
    86	            ErrorCode.title_too_long,
    87	            f"title exceeds {max_chars} characters",
    88	            {"length": len(title), "max": max_chars},
    89	        )
    90	
    91	
    92	def validate_deadline(deadline: str | None) -> None:
    93	    if deadline is None:
    94	        return
    95	    try:
    96	        date.fromisoformat(deadline)
    97	    except ValueError as exc:
    98	        raise PlannerError(
    99	            ErrorCode.validation, "deadline must be an ISO date", {"deadline": deadline}
   100	        ) from exc

   102	
   103	def validate_body(body: str, what: str) -> None:
   104	    if not body:
   105	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"{what} must be non-empty")

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/dispatch/logic/eligibility.py | sed -n '1,100p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§7.2 dispatch eligibility over a pure DispatchCandidate, plus the gating-field
     2	pending check the data layer feeds it. Stdlib + contracts only; logic never touches
     3	the DB."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from typing import Final
     8	
     9	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    10	from planner.dispatch.contracts import DispatchCandidate
    11	from planner.tickets.contracts import (
    12	    ADVANCE_TARGET,
    13	    GATING_FIELD,
    14	    STATE_ORDER,
    15	    AtCap,
    16	    TicketState,
    17	)
    18	
    19	# Position of each state in the linear pipeline; drives the "<= ceiling" comparison.
    20	_ORDER_INDEX: Final[dict[TicketState, int]] = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(STATE_ORDER)}
    21	
    22	
    23	def gating_field_pending(state: TicketState, fields: JsonDict) -> bool:
    24	    """True iff the state's gating field carries a pending proposal in the parsed
    25	    tickets.fields JSON. States without a gating field (needs_review, done, dropped)
    26	    return False."""
    27	    name = GATING_FIELD.get(state)
    28	    if name is None:
    29	        return False
    30	    slot = fields.get(name.value)
    31	    if not isinstance(slot, dict):
    32	        return False
    33	    return slot.get("proposal") is not None
    34	
    35	
    36	def is_eligible(candidate: DispatchCandidate) -> bool:
    37	    """§7.2 (SPEC line 149): the dispatch-eligibility conjunction plus the two
    38	    advance branches. Checks run in the order below; the terminal-state guard also
    39	    guarantees ADVANCE_TARGET[state] below never KeyErrors (the four remaining states
    40	    are exactly its keys)."""
    41	    if candidate.state in (
    42	        TicketState.done,
    43	        TicketState.dropped,
    44	        TicketState.needs_review,
    45	    ):
    46	        return False
    47	    if candidate.is_blocked:            # §3.6 line 57
    48	        return False
    49	    if candidate.auto_blocked:          # §7.5
    50	        return False
    51	    if candidate.has_active_claim:
    52	        return False
    53	    if candidate.gating_pending:        # current gating field has a pending proposal
    54	        return False
    55	    target = ADVANCE_TARGET[candidate.state]
    56	    if _ORDER_INDEX[target] <= _ORDER_INDEX[candidate.ceiling]:
    57	        return True                     # branch (a): the agent can move it
    58	    # branch (b): at ceiling + propose eligible; at ceiling + stop and past-ceiling not.
    59	    return candidate.state == candidate.ceiling and candidate.at_cap is AtCap.propose

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/days/logic/carryover.py | sed -n '1,150p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""The boundary deterministic pass (§6.2) as pure functions over plain projected
     2	row data. Produce the ``BoundaryInputs`` digest lists: carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals — plus the yesterday done/not-done split for ``day_closed``. Zero DB
     4	access; the boundary layer does the SQL and hands rows in. Stdlib + planner
     5	contract modules only."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from typing import Any
    10	
    11	from planner.sprints.contracts import ItemStatus
    12	from planner.tickets.contracts import GATING_FIELD, TicketState
    13	
    14	# §6.2.2 — carryover excludes tickets that are already terminal.
    15	_CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE = {TicketState.done, TicketState.dropped}
    16	# Overdue excludes items that are done or already deferred to next sprint.
    17	_ITEM_CLOSED = {ItemStatus.done, ItemStatus.deferred_next_sprint}
    18	
    19	
    20	def carryover_candidates(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    21	    """§6.2 step 2: yesterday's day-tickets whose ticket state is NOT done/dropped.
    22	
    23	    Input rows carry {id, title, state, priority} (ticket joined to its day-ticket,
    24	    in day-ticket position order). Output digest keeps the same four keys. StrEnum
    25	    members compare equal to their string values, so plain state strings work.
    26	    """
    27	    return [
    28	        {
    29	            "id": row["id"],
    30	            "title": row["title"],
    31	            "state": row["state"],
    32	            "priority": row["priority"],
    33	        }
    34	        for row in yesterday_tickets
    35	        if row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE
    36	    ]
    37	
    38	
    39	def day_ticket_counts(yesterday_tickets: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> tuple[int, int]:
    40	    """§6.2 step 4: (done_count, not_done_count) over yesterday's day-tickets.
    41	
    42	    done_count = rows with state == done; not_done_count = the rest (dropped and
    43	    every unfinished state). The two sum to len(rows).
    44	    """
    45	    done_count = sum(1 for row in yesterday_tickets if row["state"] == TicketState.done)
    46	    return done_count, len(yesterday_tickets) - done_count
    47	
    48	
    49	def overdue_list(
    50	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]], today_iso: str
    51	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    52	    """§6.2 step 3 (overdue). A ticket is overdue iff deadline is not None and
    53	    deadline < today_iso (strict; due-today is NOT overdue) and state not in
    54	    {done, dropped}. An item is overdue iff deadline < today_iso and status not
    55	    in {done, deferred_next_sprint}. Uniform digest {id, title, state, priority}
    56	    (for items, state = the item status). ISO date strings compare lexically.
    57	    Tickets first, then items."""
    58	    result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    59	    for row in tickets:
    60	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    61	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["state"] not in _CARRYOVER_EXCLUDE:
    62	            result.append(
    63	                {
    64	                    "id": row["id"],
    65	                    "title": row["title"],
    66	                    "state": row["state"],
    67	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    68	                }
    69	            )
    70	    for row in items:
    71	        deadline = row["deadline"]
    72	        if deadline is not None and deadline < today_iso and row["status"] not in _ITEM_CLOSED:
    73	            result.append(
    74	                {
    75	                    "id": row["id"],
    76	                    "title": row["title"],
    77	                    "state": row["status"],
    78	                    "priority": row["priority"],
    79	                }
    80	            )
    81	    return result
    82	
    83	
    84	def approvals_digest(
    85	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]], items: list[dict[str, Any]]
    86	) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    87	    """§6.2 step 3 / §4.5 approval-queue digest. Three sources, oldest-pending first:
    88	
    89	    * a ticket with a non-null proposal on its current gating field
    90	      (GATING_FIELD[state]); kind = the gating field name (e.g. "plan"),
    91	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"];
    92	    * a ticket in ``needs_review`` (§4.5); kind = "review", waiting_since =
    93	      the ticket's updated_at (DB-internal proxy for review entry time);
    94	    * a sprint item with a non-null status_proposal; kind = "status",
    95	      waiting_since = proposal["created_at"].
    96	
    97	    Digest per base.py: {entity_id, kind, waiting_since}. Ticket rows carry
    98	    {id, state, fields(dict), updated_at}; item rows carry {id, status_proposal}.
    99	    """
   100	    digest: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   101	    for row in tickets:
   102	        state = row["state"]
   103	        if state == TicketState.needs_review:
   104	            digest.append(
   105	                {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "review", "waiting_since": row["updated_at"]}
   106	            )
   107	            continue
   108	        gating = GATING_FIELD.get(TicketState(state))
   109	        if gating is None:
   110	            continue
   111	        slot = row["fields"].get(gating.value)
   112	        if slot is None:
   113	            continue
   114	        proposal = slot.get("proposal")
   115	        if proposal is None:
   116	            continue
   117	        digest.append(
   118	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": gating.value, "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   119	        )
   120	    for row in items:
   121	        proposal = row["status_proposal"]
   122	        if proposal is None:
   123	            continue
   124	        digest.append(
   125	            {"entity_id": row["id"], "kind": "status", "waiting_since": proposal["created_at"]}
   126	        )
   127	    digest.sort(key=lambda entry: entry["waiting_since"])
   128	    return digest

     1	"""The boundary job (§6.2). Runs once per planning date, guarded by the
     2	boundary_runs table. Deterministic pass (materialize the day, carryover, overdue,
     3	approvals, close yesterday) then one adapter judgment call wrapped in a
     4	caller-owned timeout. On failure/timeout the day survives with empty brief and no
     5	plan. Judgment is skipped entirely when the human already planned the day."""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import sqlite3
    11	from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    12	from concurrent.futures import TimeoutError as FuturesTimeout
    13	from datetime import timedelta
    14	from typing import Any
    15	
    16	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, BoundaryInputs, BoundaryJudgment
    17	from planner.core.clock import Clock
    18	from planner.core.config import Config
    19	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind
    20	from planner.core.events import append_event
    21	from planner.core.ids import day_id
    22	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus
    23	from planner.days.data import load_plan, materialize_day, store_judgment
    24	from planner.days.logic.carryover import (
    25	    approvals_digest,
    26	    carryover_candidates,
    27	    day_ticket_counts,
    28	    overdue_list,
    29	)
    30	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date
    31	from planner.days.logic.tree import as_proposed, tree_to_dict
    32	
    33	
    34	def run_boundary(
    35	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, clock: Clock, config: Config, adapter: BoundaryAdapter
    36	) -> None:
    37	    """One boundary tick. Idempotent per planning date via the boundary_runs
    38	    guard, so the stage-4 scheduler may call it every tick — only the first tick
    39	    that advances the planning date does work (planning_date only becomes the new
    40	    date at/after boundary_hour, satisfying §6.2 'first tick at/after the boundary
    41	    hour')."""
    42	    pd = planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour)
    43	    piso = pd.isoformat()
    44	    if _boundary_ran(conn, piso):
    45	        return
    46	
    47	    now = clock.now_unix()
    48	    ndid = day_id(pd)
    49	    yid = day_id(pd - timedelta(days=1))
    50	
    51	    # Deterministic pass — always runs on a first tick.
    52	    materialize_day(conn, ndid, now)
    53	    yts = _read_yesterday_tickets(conn, yid)
    54	    carry = carryover_candidates(yts)
    55	    overdue_tickets, overdue_items = _read_overdue_candidates(conn)
    56	    overdue = overdue_list(overdue_tickets, overdue_items, piso)
    57	    approval_tickets, approval_items = _read_approval_candidates(conn)
    58	    approvals = approvals_digest(approval_tickets, approval_items)
    59	    done, not_done = day_ticket_counts(yts)
    60	    append_event(
    61	        conn,
    62	        yid,
    63	        EventKind.day_closed,
    64	        {"done_count": done, "not_done_count": not_done},
    65	        now,
    66	    )
    67	
    68	    # Skip check (§6.2 last paragraph): a human already planned this day.
    69	    if _human_planned(conn, ndid):
    70	        _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "skipped")
    71	        return
    72	
    73	    # Judgment pass — one adapter call under a caller-owned timeout.
    74	    inputs = BoundaryInputs(
    75	        planning_date=piso, carryover=carry, overdue=overdue, approvals_digest=approvals
    76	    )
    77	    timeout_s = config.boundary_timeout_seconds
    78	    try:
    79	        judgment = _judgment_with_timeout(adapter, inputs, timeout_s)
    80	    except Exception as exc:  # timeout OR any adapter raise; day survives empty
    81	        append_event(
    82	            conn, ndid, EventKind.boundary_failed, {"error": _error_text(exc, timeout_s)}, now
    83	        )
    84	        _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "failed")
    85	        return
    86	
    87	    proposed = as_proposed(judgment.plan_tree)  # §6.2 stored as a PROPOSED plan
    88	    store_judgment(conn, ndid, judgment.brief_markdown, proposed, now)
    89	    append_event(conn, ndid, EventKind.plan_proposed, {"tree": tree_to_dict(proposed)}, now)
    90	    _record_boundary(conn, piso, now, "ok")
    91	
    92	
    93	def _boundary_ran(conn: sqlite3.Connection, pd_iso: str) -> bool:
    94	    return conn.execute(
    95	        "SELECT 1 FROM boundary_runs WHERE planning_date = ?", (pd_iso,)
    96	    ).fetchone() is not None
    97	
    98	
    99	def _record_boundary(conn: sqlite3.Connection, pd_iso: str, now_unix: int, judgment: str) -> None:
   100	    conn.execute(
   101	        "INSERT INTO boundary_runs (planning_date, ran_at, judgment) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
   102	        (pd_iso, now_unix, judgment),
   103	    )
   104	
   105	
   106	def _read_yesterday_tickets(conn: sqlite3.Connection, yesterday_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   107	    rows = conn.execute(
   108	        "SELECT t.id AS id, t.title AS title, t.state AS state, t.priority AS priority "
   109	        "FROM day_tickets dt JOIN tickets t ON t.id = dt.ticket_id "
   110	        "WHERE dt.day_id = ? ORDER BY dt.position",
   111	        (yesterday_id,),
   112	    ).fetchall()
   113	    return [
   114	        {"id": r["id"], "title": r["title"], "state": r["state"], "priority": r["priority"]}
   115	        for r in rows
   116	    ]
   117	
   118	
   119	def _read_overdue_candidates(
   120	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   121	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   122	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   123	        {
   124	            "id": r["id"],
   125	            "title": r["title"],
   126	            "state": r["state"],
   127	            "priority": r["priority"],
   128	            "deadline": r["deadline"],
   129	        }
   130	        for r in conn.execute(
   131	            "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline FROM tickets"
   132	        ).fetchall()
   133	    ]
   134	    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   135	        {
   136	            "id": r["id"],
   137	            "title": r["title"],
   138	            "status": r["status"],
   139	            "priority": r["priority"],
   140	            "deadline": r["deadline"],
   141	        }
   142	        for r in conn.execute(
   143	            "SELECT id, title, status, priority, deadline FROM sprint_items"
   144	        ).fetchall()
   145	    ]
   146	    return tickets, items
   147	
   148	
   149	def _read_approval_candidates(
   150	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   151	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
   152	    tickets: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
   153	        {
   154	            "id": r["id"],
   155	            "state": r["state"],
   156	            "fields": json.loads(r["fields"]),
   157	            "updated_at": r["updated_at"],
   158	        }
   159	        for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets").fetchall()
   160	    ]
   161	    items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   162	    for r in conn.execute("SELECT id, status_proposal FROM sprint_items").fetchall():
   163	        raw = r["status_proposal"]
   164	        items.append(
   165	            {"id": r["id"], "status_proposal": json.loads(raw) if raw is not None else None}
   166	        )
   167	    return tickets, items
   168	
   169	
   170	def _human_planned(conn: sqlite3.Connection, new_day_id: str) -> bool:
   171	    """True iff a day-ticket exists on the new day OR the stored plan has the root
   172	    accepted or any child accepted (§6.2 'any day-ticket or accepted plan')."""
   173	    if conn.execute(
   174	        "SELECT 1 FROM day_tickets WHERE day_id = ? LIMIT 1", (new_day_id,)
   175	    ).fetchone() is not None:
   176	        return True
   177	    tree = load_plan(conn, new_day_id)
   178	    if tree is None:
   179	        return False
   180	    if tree.root.status == NodeStatus.accepted:
   181	        return True
   182	    return any(child.status == NodeStatus.accepted for child in tree.children)
   183	
   184	
   185	def _judgment_with_timeout(
   186	    adapter: BoundaryAdapter, inputs: BoundaryInputs, timeout_s: int
   187	) -> BoundaryJudgment:
   188	    """Run adapter.judgment in a worker thread; the CALLER owns the timeout
   189	    (base.py). result(timeout=...) raises FuturesTimeout on timeout and re-raises
   190	    any adapter exception — both are caught by run_boundary. shutdown(wait=False)
   191	    so a hung adapter cannot block run_boundary past the configured timeout; the
   192	    abandoned worker thread is left to finish on its own."""
   193	    executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)
   194	    try:
   195	        return executor.submit(adapter.judgment, inputs).result(timeout=timeout_s)
   196	    finally:
   197	        executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
   198	
   199	
   200	def _error_text(exc: Exception, timeout_s: int) -> str:
   201	    if isinstance(exc, FuturesTimeout):
   202	        return f"boundary judgment timed out after {timeout_s}s"
   203	    return str(exc) or exc.__class__.__name__

   130	  ticket_id  TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tickets(id),
   131	  status     TEXT NOT NULL
   132	             CHECK (status IN ('running','done','blocked','crashed','timed_out','reclaimed','spawn_failed')),
   133	  started_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
   134	  ended_at   INTEGER,
   135	  summary    TEXT,
   136	  error      TEXT,
   137	  pid        INTEGER
   138	);
   139	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_ticket ON runs(ticket_id, started_at);
   140	
   141	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS boundary_runs (           -- §6.2: never twice per date
   142	  planning_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                    -- ISO date
   143	  ran_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
   144	  judgment      TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (judgment IN ('ok','skipped','failed'))
   145	);
   146	"""
   147	
   148	
   149	def connect(db_path: str, busy_timeout_ms: int = 5000) -> sqlite3.Connection:
   150	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, isolation_level=None)
   151	    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   152	    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
   153	    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
   154	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA busy_timeout={int(busy_timeout_ms)}")
   155	    return conn
   156	
   157	
   158	def create_schema(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
   159	    conn.executescript(DDL)
   160	    conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version={SCHEMA_VERSION}")

   167	    day_updated {field: "brief"}."""

   176	    """Manual notes edit: materialize, UPDATE notes + updated_at, append
   177	    day_updated {field: "notes"}."""
   178	    materialize_day(conn, day_id, now_unix)
   179	    conn.execute(
   180	        "UPDATE days SET notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (notes, now_unix, day_id)
   181	    )
   182	    append_event(conn, day_id, EventKind.day_updated, {"field": "notes"}, now_unix)

   184	
   185	def apply_plan_effects(
   186	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   187	    day_id: str,
   188	    new_tree: PlanTree | None,
   189	    effects: list[Effect],
   190	    now_unix: int,
   191	) -> list[ReplanRequest]:
   192	    """Persist the transform result and apply its effects. (1) store_plan writes
   193	    days.plan (or NULL for reject-all). (2) For each effect in order:
   194	    AddTicketToDay → add_day_ticket(cause="plan_accept") (idempotent);
   195	    EmitEvent → append_event; ReplanRoot/ReplanChild → collected. (3) Return the
   196	    collected replan requests for the stage-4 runtime to serialize + execute (R5).
   197	    At stage 3 no adapter is called here."""
   198	    store_plan(conn, day_id, new_tree, now_unix)
   199	    replans: list[ReplanRequest] = []
   200	    for effect in effects:
   201	        if isinstance(effect, AddTicketToDay):
   202	            add_day_ticket(conn, day_id, effect.ticket_id, now_unix, cause="plan_accept")
   203	        elif isinstance(effect, EmitEvent):
   204	            append_event(conn, day_id, effect.kind, effect.payload, now_unix)
   205	        elif isinstance(effect, (ReplanRoot, ReplanChild)):
   206	            replans.append(effect)
   207	    return replans

    52	def resolve_day_id(date_seg: str, clock: Clock, config: Config) -> str:
    53	    """Amendment 10: `today` resolves through the planning date. A6: the ISO branch
    54	    parses then re-formats via ids.day_id, so compact forms ('20260704') still yield
    55	    canonical day_2026-07-04 ids — the raw segment is never string-formatted."""
    56	    if date_seg == "today":
    57	        return ids.day_id(planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour))
    58	    try:
    59	        parsed = date.fromisoformat(date_seg)
    60	    except ValueError:
    61	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid date", {"date": date_seg}) from None
    62	    return ids.day_id(parsed)

    64	
    65	def _day_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
    66	    # A2: read_day materializes an absent day (INSERT + event, multi-statement) on an
    67	    # autocommit connection, so this one read is wrapped; the rest run bare.
    68	    with txn(conn):
    69	        day = days_data.read_day(conn, did, now)
    70	    dts = days_data.list_day_tickets(conn, did)
    71	    return {
    72	        "id": day.id,
    73	        "brief": day.brief,
    74	        "notes": day.notes,
    75	        "plan": plan_tree.tree_to_dict(day.plan) if day.plan is not None else None,
    76	        "chat_session_key": day.chat_session_key,
    77	        "created_at": day.created_at,
    78	        "updated_at": day.updated_at,
    79	        "tickets": [ticket_json(read_ticket(conn, dt.ticket_id), now) for dt in dts],
    80	    }
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_node(raw: object) -> str | int:
    84	    if raw == "root":
    85	        return "root"
    86	    if isinstance(raw, int) and not isinstance(raw, bool):
    87	        return raw
    88	    raise PlannerError(
    89	        ErrorCode.validation, "node must be 'root' or a child position", {"node": raw}
    90	    )
    91	
    92	
    93	def _load_plan_or_error(conn: sqlite3.Connection, did: str) -> PlanTree:
    94	    plan = days_data.load_plan(conn, did)
    95	    if plan is None:
    96	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "day has no plan", {"day_id": did})
    97	    return plan
    98	
    99	
   100	def _require_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> None:
   101	    if not any(child.position == position for child in tree.children):
   102	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no such plan node", {"node": position})

   108	@router.get("/day/{date}")

   110	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   111	    return _day_view(conn, did, clk.now_unix())

   114	@router.patch("/day/{date}")
   115	async def patch_day(date: str, body: DayPatchBody, conn: DbConn, cfg: Cfg, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   116	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   117	    now = clk.now_unix()
   118	    if body.brief is None and body.notes is None:
   119	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no day fields to update", {})
   120	    if body.brief is not None:
   121	        with txn(conn):
   122	            days_data.set_brief(conn, did, body.brief, now)
   123	    if body.notes is not None:
   124	        with txn(conn):
   125	            days_data.set_notes(conn, did, body.notes, now)
   126	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

   148	
   149	
   150	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept")
   151	async def plan_accept(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   152	                      clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   153	    reject_agents(ctx)
   154	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   155	    now = clk.now_unix()
   156	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   157	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   158	    if isinstance(node, int):
   159	        _require_child(tree, node)
   160	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_node(tree, node)
   161	    with txn(conn):
   162	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   163	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   164

   166	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/accept-all")

   168	    reject_agents(ctx)
   169	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   170	    now = clk.now_unix()
   171	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   172	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.accept_all(tree)
   173	    with txn(conn):
   174	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)

   176

   179	async def plan_invalidate(date: str, body: PlanNodeBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, cfg: Cfg,
   180	                          clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   181	    reject_agents(ctx)
   182	    did = resolve_day_id(date, clk, cfg)
   183	    now = clk.now_unix()
   184	    node = _parse_node(body.node)
   185	    tree = _load_plan_or_error(conn, did)
   186	    if isinstance(node, int):
   187	        _require_child(tree, node)
   188	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_child(tree, node)
   189	    else:
   190	        new_tree, effects = plan_tree.invalidate_root(tree)
   191	    with txn(conn):
   192	        replans = days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   193	    for request in replans:  # post-commit enqueue: the R5 consumer sees committed state only
   194	        submit_replan(did, request)
   195	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)
   196	
   197	
   198	@router.post("/day/{date}/plan/reject-all")

   204	    new_tree, effects = plan_tree.reject_all(tree)
   205	    with txn(conn):
   206	        days_data.apply_plan_effects(conn, did, new_tree, effects, now)
   207	    return _day_view(conn, did, now)

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba src/planner/days/logic/tree.py | sed -n '1,160p'" in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Plan-tree transforms (§6.3) as pure functions ``PlanTree → (new_tree, effects)``,
     2	plus JSON serialization of the tree for the ``days.plan`` column and for event
     3	payloads that carry ``old_tree``. No side effects; the data layer persists the
     4	returned tree and applies the effects. Stdlib + planner contract modules only."""
     5	
     6	from __future__ import annotations
     7	
     8	from dataclasses import replace
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, JsonDict
    11	from planner.days.contracts import NodeStatus, PlanNode, PlanRoot, PlanTree
    12	from planner.days.logic.effects import (
    13	    AddTicketToDay,
    14	    Effect,
    15	    EmitEvent,
    16	    ReplanChild,
    17	    ReplanRoot,
    18	)
    19	
    20	# A node address in the plan tree: the literal ``"root"`` or a child ``position``.
    21	# Mirrors the plan_node_accepted / plan_node_invalidated payload contract, whose
    22	# ``{node}`` value is ``"root" | <child position>``.
    23	NodeRef = str | int
    24	
    25	
    26	def tree_to_dict(tree: PlanTree) -> JsonDict:
    27	    """{root:{focus,status}, children:[{ticket_id,note,status,position}, ...]}.
    28	
    29	    NodeStatus serialized as its str value; child order preserved.
    30	    """
    31	    return {
    32	        "root": {"focus": tree.root.focus, "status": tree.root.status.value},
    33	        "children": [
    34	            {
    35	                "ticket_id": child.ticket_id,
    36	                "note": child.note,
    37	                "status": child.status.value,
    38	                "position": child.position,
    39	            }
    40	            for child in tree.children
    41	        ],
    42	    }
    43	
    44	
    45	def tree_from_dict(data: JsonDict) -> PlanTree:
    46	    """Inverse of ``tree_to_dict``. status parsed via ``NodeStatus(...)``."""
    47	    root = data["root"]
    48	    children = [
    49	        PlanNode(
    50	            ticket_id=child["ticket_id"],
    51	            note=child["note"],
    52	            status=NodeStatus(child["status"]),
    53	            position=child["position"],
    54	        )
    55	        for child in data["children"]
    56	    ]
    57	    return PlanTree(
    58	        root=PlanRoot(focus=root["focus"], status=NodeStatus(root["status"])),
    59	        children=children,
    60	    )
    61	
    62	
    63	def as_proposed(tree: PlanTree) -> PlanTree:
    64	    """Return a copy with root and every child status=proposed. The boundary
    65	    stores the judgment tree as a PROPOSED plan (§6.2)."""
    66	    return PlanTree(
    67	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.proposed),
    68	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.proposed) for child in tree.children],
    69	    )
    70	
    71	
    72	def accept_node(tree: PlanTree, ref: NodeRef) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
    73	    """§6.3: accepting a node sets it accepted; accepting root does NOT cascade.
    74	
    75	    A child with a ticket_id also yields an AddTicketToDay effect (appended at
    76	    end if absent). Effect order: EmitEvent(plan_node_accepted) then (for a child
    77	    with a ticket_id) AddTicketToDay.
    78	    """
    79	    if ref == "root":
    80	        new_tree = PlanTree(
    81	            root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
    82	            children=[replace(child) for child in tree.children],
    83	        )
    84	        return new_tree, [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": "root"})]
    85	
    86	    accepted: PlanNode | None = None
    87	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
    88	    for child in tree.children:
    89	        if child.position == ref:
    90	            accepted = replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted)
    91	            children.append(accepted)
    92	        else:
    93	            children.append(replace(child))
    94	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
    95	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_accepted, {"node": ref})]
    96	    if accepted is not None and accepted.ticket_id:
    97	        effects.append(AddTicketToDay(accepted.ticket_id))
    98	    return new_tree, effects
    99	
   100	
   101	def accept_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   102	    """§6.3 accept-all: root + every child accepted. Effects =
   103	    [EmitEvent(plan_accepted_all, {})] then AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id) for
   104	    each child with a ticket_id, in child position order. Idempotent add is the
   105	    data layer's job (a17)."""
   106	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   107	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.accepted),
   108	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.accepted) for child in tree.children],
   109	    )
   110	    effects: list[Effect] = [EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_accepted_all, {})]
   111	    for child in tree.children:
   112	        if child.ticket_id:
   113	            effects.append(AddTicketToDay(child.ticket_id))
   114	    return new_tree, effects
   115	
   116	
   117	def invalidate_root(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   118	    """§6.3: root + EVERY child → invalidated; request exactly one root replan."""
   119	    new_tree = PlanTree(
   120	        root=replace(tree.root, status=NodeStatus.invalidated),
   121	        children=[replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated) for child in tree.children],
   122	    )
   123	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   124	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": "root"}),
   125	        ReplanRoot(),
   126	    ]
   127	    return new_tree, effects
   128	
   129	
   130	def invalidate_child(tree: PlanTree, position: int) -> tuple[PlanTree, list[Effect]]:
   131	    """§6.3: ONLY the child at ``position`` → invalidated; root and every other
   132	    child keep their prior status. Requests exactly one child replan."""
   133	    children: list[PlanNode] = []
   134	    for child in tree.children:
   135	        if child.position == position:
   136	            children.append(replace(child, status=NodeStatus.invalidated))
   137	        else:
   138	            children.append(replace(child))
   139	    new_tree = PlanTree(root=replace(tree.root), children=children)
   140	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   141	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_node_invalidated, {"node": position}),
   142	        ReplanChild(position),
   143	    ]
   144	    return new_tree, effects
   145	
   146	
   147	def reject_all(tree: PlanTree) -> tuple[None, list[Effect]]:
   148	    """§6.3 reject-all: plan cleared. New tree is None (the data layer writes
   149	    days.plan = NULL). The old tree is preserved in the event payload."""
   150	    effects: list[Effect] = [
   151	        EmitEvent(EventKind.plan_rejected, {"old_tree": tree_to_dict(tree)})
   152	    ]
   153	    return None, effects

     1	"""The clock. Real time in production; a mutable fake in test mode so planning-date
     2	math (§6.1) and the set-now test endpoint (D5) are deterministic. The choice is
     3	made once at startup from config."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from datetime import datetime
     8	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Protocol
     9	
    10	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    11	    from planner.core.config import Config
    12	
    13	
    14	class Clock(Protocol):
    15	    def now(self) -> datetime: ...       # timezone-aware, LOCAL time (§6.1 math is local)
    16	    def now_unix(self) -> int: ...
    17	
    18	
    19	class RealClock:
    20	    def now(self) -> datetime:
    21	        return datetime.now().astimezone()
    22	
    23	    def now_unix(self) -> int:
    24	        return int(self.now().timestamp())
    25	
    26	
    27	class TestClock:
    28	    """Mutable clock for PLAN_FAKE_NOW and POST /api/test/set-now (D5)."""
    29	
    30	    def __init__(self, start: datetime) -> None:
    31	        self._now = start
    32	
    33	    def now(self) -> datetime:
    34	        return self._now
    35	
    36	    def now_unix(self) -> int:
    37	        return int(self._now.timestamp())
    38	
    39	    def set(self, now: datetime) -> None:
    40	        self._now = now
    41	
    42	
    43	def parse_fake_now(raw: str) -> datetime:
    44	    """PLAN_FAKE_NOW is ISO; a naive value is interpreted as local time."""
    45	    parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(raw)
    46	    if parsed.tzinfo is None:
    47	        parsed = parsed.astimezone()
    48	    return parsed
    49	
    50	
    51	def build_clock(config: Config) -> Clock:
    52	    if config.test_mode and config.fake_now:
    53	        return TestClock(parse_fake_now(config.fake_now))
    54	    return RealClock()

    79	@contextmanager
    80	def txn(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> Iterator[None]:
    81	    """Wrap a NON-self-transacting writer (days/dispatch/links) so a mid-sequence
    82	    PlannerError rolls back cleanly. Never wrap a ticket/sprint writer — those open
    83	    their own BEGIN IMMEDIATE and would raise 'transaction within a transaction'."""
    84	    conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    85	    try:
    86	        yield
    87	    except BaseException:
    88	        conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    89	        raise
    90	    else:
    91	        conn.execute("COMMIT")

    97	    except ValueError:
    98	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {what}", {what: raw}) from None

     1	"""The only module that writes or reads the events table. There is no update or
     2	delete path anywhere: events are append-only (§3). The read limit is always
     3	passed in by the caller (from config); this module stays config-free."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	import json
     8	import sqlite3
     9	
    10	from planner.core.contracts import EventKind, EventRow, JsonDict
    11	
    12	
    13	def append_event(
    14	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    15	    entity_id: str,
    16	    kind: EventKind,
    17	    payload: JsonDict,
    18	    created_at: int,
    19	) -> int:
    20	    cursor = conn.execute(
    21	        "INSERT INTO events (entity_id, kind, payload, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
    22	        (entity_id, kind.value, json.dumps(payload), created_at),
    23	    )
    24	    inserted = cursor.lastrowid
    25	    assert inserted is not None
    26	    return inserted
    27	
    28	
    29	def read_events_since(conn: sqlite3.Connection, since_id: int, limit: int) -> list[EventRow]:
    30	    rows = conn.execute(
    31	        "SELECT id, entity_id, kind, payload, created_at "
    32	        "FROM events WHERE id > ? ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
    33	        (since_id, limit),
    34	    ).fetchall()
    35	    return [
    36	        EventRow(
    37	            id=row["id"],
    38	            entity_id=row["entity_id"],
    39	            kind=row["kind"],
    40	            payload=json.loads(row["payload"]),
    41	            created_at=row["created_at"],
    42	        )
    43	        for row in rows
    44	    ]

     1	"""§9/§13 test-mode control router. Mounted under /api only when config.test_mode,
     2	so /api/test/* is a plain 404 otherwise (the router is never built). set-now drives
     3	the TestClock; the two tick endpoints call T11's runtime through a lazy import seam
     4	and answer 501 until it lands.
     5	
     6	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py family)."""
     7	
     8	from __future__ import annotations
     9	
    10	import importlib
    11	import sqlite3
    12	from collections.abc import Callable
    13	from typing import Any
    14	
    15	from fastapi import APIRouter
    16	from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
    17	
    18	from planner.core.adapters.registry import Adapters
    19	from planner.core.clock import Clock, TestClock, parse_fake_now
    20	from planner.core.config import Config
    21	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	from planner.days.logic.dates import planning_date

    25	
    26	def _not_wired() -> JSONResponse:
    27	    # Returned directly (never raised as a PlannerError, which the handler would map
    28	    # to 400): the seam pins this exact body for T11's tests to compare whole.
    29	    return JSONResponse(
    30	        status_code=501,
    31	        content={"error": {"code": "validation", "message": "runtime not wired yet"}},
    32	    )
    33	
    34	
    35	def _lazy(module_name: str, attr: str) -> Any | None:
    36	    # importlib (not a from-import) keeps the not-yet-existing T11 modules out of the
    37	    # static import graph; ImportError/AttributeError are the seam's named failures.
    38	    try:
    39	        module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
    40	        return getattr(module, attr)
    41	    except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    42	        return None
    43	
    44	
    45	def build_test_router(
    46	    config: Config,
    47	    clock: Clock,
    48	    adapters: Adapters,
    49	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    50	) -> APIRouter:
    51	    router = APIRouter()
    52	
    53	    @router.post("/test/set-now")
    54	    async def set_now(body: dict[str, Any]) -> JsonDict:
    55	        raw = body.get("now")
    56	        if not isinstance(raw, str) or not raw.strip():
    57	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "body must carry an ISO 'now' string")
    58	        try:
    59	            parsed = parse_fake_now(raw)
    60	        except ValueError as exc:
    61	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid ISO datetime: {raw!r}") from exc
    62	        if not isinstance(clock, TestClock):
    63	            raise PlannerError(
    64	                ErrorCode.validation, "clock is not a TestClock; start with PLAN_FAKE_NOW set"
    65	            )
    66	        clock.set(parsed)
    67	        return {
    68	            "now": clock.now().isoformat(),
    69	            "planning_date": planning_date(clock.now(), config.boundary_hour).isoformat(),
    70	        }
    71	
    72	    @router.post("/test/tick-boundary")
    73	    async def tick_boundary() -> JSONResponse:
    74	        fn = _lazy("planner.days.scheduler", "run_boundary_tick")
    75	        if fn is None:
    76	            return _not_wired()
    77	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    78	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)
    79	
    80	    @router.post("/test/tick-dispatcher")
    81	    async def tick_dispatcher() -> JSONResponse:
    82	        fn = _lazy("planner.dispatch.runtime", "run_tick")
    83	        if fn is None:
    84	            return _not_wired()
    85	        report = fn(conn_factory, config, clock, adapters)
    86	        return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content=report)
    87	
    88	    return router

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Adapter registry (§9.4). Selects real vs fake (vs offline gateway) per adapter
     2	from config keys. `auto` resolves to fake under test mode and real otherwise;
     3	explicit values always win. An unknown value fails loudly at startup."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from dataclasses import dataclass
     8	from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
     9	
    10	from planner.core.adapters.base import BoundaryAdapter, GatewayAdapter, SpawnAdapter
    11	from planner.core.adapters.fakes import (
    12	    EchoGatewayAdapter,
    13	    FakeBoundaryAdapter,
    14	    FakeSpawnAdapter,
    15	    OfflineGatewayAdapter,
    16	)
    17	from planner.core.adapters.real import (
    18	    RealBoundaryAdapter,
    19	    RealGatewayAdapter,
    20	    RealSpawnAdapter,
    21	)
    22	from planner.core.errors import ErrorCode, PlannerError
    23	
    24	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    25	    from planner.core.config import Config
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    29	class Adapters:
    30	    spawn: SpawnAdapter
    31	    boundary: BoundaryAdapter
    32	    gateway: GatewayAdapter
    33	
    34	
    35	def _resolve_auto(choice: str, test_mode: bool) -> str:
    36	    if choice == "auto":
    37	        return "fake" if test_mode else "real"
    38	    return choice
    39	
    40	
    41	def _select_spawn(config: Config) -> SpawnAdapter:
    42	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.spawn_adapter, config.test_mode)
    43	    if choice == "fake":
    44	        return FakeSpawnAdapter()
    45	    if choice == "real":
    46	        return RealSpawnAdapter(config)
    47	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown spawn_adapter: {config.spawn_adapter!r}")
    48	
    49	
    50	def _select_boundary(config: Config) -> BoundaryAdapter:
    51	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.boundary_adapter, config.test_mode)
    52	    if choice == "fake":
    53	        return FakeBoundaryAdapter()
    54	    if choice == "real":
    55	        return RealBoundaryAdapter(config)
    56	    raise PlannerError(
    57	        ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown boundary_adapter: {config.boundary_adapter!r}"
    58	    )
    59	
    60	
    61	def _select_gateway(config: Config) -> GatewayAdapter:
    62	    choice = _resolve_auto(config.gateway_adapter, config.test_mode)
    63	    if choice == "fake":
    64	        return EchoGatewayAdapter()
    65	    if choice == "offline":
    66	        return OfflineGatewayAdapter()
    67	    if choice == "real":
    68	        return RealGatewayAdapter(config)
    69	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"unknown gateway_adapter: {config.gateway_adapter!r}")
    70	
    71	
    72	def build_adapters(config: Config) -> Adapters:
    73	    return Adapters(
    74	        spawn=_select_spawn(config),
    75	        boundary=_select_boundary(config),
    76	        gateway=_select_gateway(config),
    77	    )

The replan ticks do not assert the full pinned 4-key report; root replan omits `planning_date`, `ran`, and `judgment`, and child replan omits `planning_date`. Why: SPEC fence requires exact values, not weakened approximations (SPEC.md:269-272); `run_boundary_tick` returns pinned `{planning_date, ran, judgment, replan}` (src/planner/days/scheduler.py:246-273); plan says second same-date ticks pin `ran False`, `judgment "ok"` (plan.md:542-544). Fix: assert the full dict for both replan ticks, with the expected `replan.attempts`.

[test_e30/279-284, test_e31/356-358] Dispatcher tick report checks are weakened. They do not assert the pinned 5-key shape, empty `reclaimed`/`timed_out`/`spawn_failed`, or fake spawn `pid`. Why: `run_tick` returns pinned keys (src/planner/dispatch/runtime.py:123-133), spawn entries include `pid` (runtime.py:197-199), and the fake adapter’s first pid is `90001` (src/planner/core/adapters/fakes.py:18, 27-33). Fix: assert `set(rep)`, the three empty lists, `skipped is None`, one spawned row with `ticket_id`, truthy `run_id`, and `pid == 90001`.

[test_e30/297-302, 325-336] The claimed `propose result` path is not proven to store/render `E30_RESULT`; the test only asserts state and queue/approval. Why: SPEC item 30 requires the fake worker’s claimed CLI result proposal to park the ticket in `needs_review` (SPEC.md:309); the source auto-accept path stores the proposal body as the result value (src/planner/tickets/logic/resolution.py:101-118, 41-55), and the review card renders `detail.fields.result.value` (assets/components.js:718-722). Fix: after line 301 assert `r["fields"]["result"]["value"] == E30_RESULT` and `r["fields"]["result"]["proposal"] is None`; before approve, assert the review card body contains or equals `E30_RESULT`.
